````tsx
// Hosts (frontend spec §8, kernel spec §4): every paired host, revoked ones
// included, with its online state and versions; pairing a new one; and
// renaming, re-hatting or revoking one, each confirmed, then stepped up.
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { messageOf } from '../api/errors'
import { hats as listHats, hosts as listHosts, mintPairingCode, revokeHost, settings, updateHost } from '../api/manage'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import ConfirmDialog from '../components/ConfirmDialog'
import Pairing, { type Minted } from '../components/Pairing'
import type { HatItem, HostItem } from '../generated/protocol'
import { useResource } from '../hooks/useResource'
import { HOST_STATE_LABEL, hostState } from '../lib/manage'
import { Text, visible } from '../lib/text'
import When from '../components/When'
import '../manage.css'

/** Each carries where focus goes when the confirmation closes and what
 *  opened it is gone. */
type Pending =
  | { kind: 'rename'; host: HostItem; name: string; back: () => HTMLElement | null }
  | { kind: 'default_hat'; host: HostItem; hat: HatItem; back: () => HTMLElement | null }
  | { kind: 'revoke'; host: HostItem; back: () => HTMLElement | null }

/** The pairing panel: a new one per code (`n`), and the code itself until
 *  it is spent. */
interface Panel {
  n: number
  minted: Minted | null
}

export default function Hosts() {
  const client = useClient()
  const list = useResource(() => listHosts(client))
  const hatList = useResource(() => listHats(client))
  const [panel, setPanel] = useState<Panel | null>(null)
  const minted = panel?.minted ?? null
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false)
  const [addError, setAddError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<Pending | null>(null)

  // The URL and the hosts already paired are read first: a failed read then
  // spends no code, and a host that pairs is told from the ones before it
  // even when the list above never loaded.
  const add = async () => {
    setAdding(true)
    setAddError(null)
    try {
      const s = await settings(client)
      const known = (await listHosts(client)).map((h) => h.host_id)
      const code = await mintPairingCode(client)
      setPanel((p) => ({ n: (p?.n ?? 0) + 1, minted: { code: code.code, publicUrl: s.public_url, known } }))
    } catch (err) {
      setAddError(messageOf(err))
    } finally {
      setAdding(false)
    }
  }

  // A page restored from the back-forward cache must not show a code: it
  // is dropped as the page is hidden. Switching tabs to paste it keeps it.
  useEffect(() => {
    const drop = () => setPanel(null)
    window.addEventListener('pagehide', drop)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('pagehide', drop)
  }, [])

  const replace = (host: HostItem) => list.set((prev) => (prev ?? []).map((h) => (h.host_id === host.host_id ? host : h)))

  const hatName = (id: string) => hatList.data?.find((h) => h.id === id)?.name

  return (
    <div className="manage">
      <header className="manage-head">
        <h1>Hosts</h1>
        <button type="button" className="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onClick={add} disabled={adding || minted !== null}>
          Add host
        </button>
      </header>
      <p className="manage-subtitle">{list.data ? `${list.data.filter(h => !h.revoked_at && h.connected).length} online · ${list.data.filter(h => !h.revoked_at && !h.connected).length} offline` : 'Loading hosts…'} · Pair machines and choose their fallback hat.</p>
      {addError && (
        <p className="form-error" role="alert">
          <Text>{addError}</Text>
        </p>
      )}
      {panel && (
        <Pairing
          key={panel.n}
          minted={panel.minted}
          onPaired={list.reload}
          onSpent={() => setPanel((p) => p && { ...p, minted: null })}
          onClose={() => setPanel(null)}
        />
      )}
      {list.error && (
        <p className="form-error" role="alert">
          <Text>{list.error}</Text>
        </p>
      )}
      {hatList.error && (
        <p className="form-error" role="alert">
          <Text>{hatList.error}</Text>
        </p>
      )}
      {list.data && list.data.length === 0 && <p className="empty">No host is paired yet.</p>}
      <ul className="cards" aria-label="Hosts">
        {(list.data ?? []).map((host) => (
          <HostCard
            key={host.host_id}
            host={host}
            hats={hatList.data ?? []}
            hatName={hatName(host.default_hat_id)}
            onRename={(name, back) => setPending({ kind: 'rename', host, name, back })}
            onDefaultHat={(hat, back) => setPending({ kind: 'default_hat', host, hat, back })}
            onRevoke={(back) => setPending({ kind: 'revoke', host, back })}
          />
        ))}
      </ul>
      {pending?.kind === 'rename' && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title="Rename this host?"
          confirm="Rename"
          action={async () => replace(await updateHost(client, pending.host.host_id, { name: pending.name }))}
          onClose={() => setPending(null)}
          returnFocus={pending.back}
        >
          <p>
            <Text>{pending.host.name}</Text> becomes <Text>{pending.name}</Text>.
          </p>
        </ConfirmDialog>
      )}
      {pending?.kind === 'default_hat' && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title="Change this host’s default hat?"
          confirm="Change"
          action={async () => replace(await updateHost(client, pending.host.host_id, { default_hat_id: pending.hat.id }))}
          onClose={() => setPending(null)}
          returnFocus={pending.back}
        >
          <p>
            Sessions on <Text>{pending.host.name}</Text> that no path rule covers will belong to{' '}
            <Text>{pending.hat.name}</Text>.
          </p>
          <p>
            Parked or closed sessions there that no rule covers keep their hat: resuming one is refused until it is
            re-assigned.
          </p>
        </ConfirmDialog>
      )}
      {pending?.kind === 'revoke' && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title="Revoke this host?"
          confirm="Revoke"
          danger
          action={async () => replace(await revokeHost(client, pending.host.host_id))}
          onClose={() => setPending(null)}
          returnFocus={pending.back}
        >
          <p>
            <Text>{pending.host.name}</Text> can no longer connect, and its sessions are parked. Pairing it again
            needs a new code.
          </p>
          <p>Agents it is running stop only when it next connects: it is then told it is revoked.</p>
        </ConfirmDialog>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

/** Where focus goes when a confirmation closes and what opened it is
 *  gone. */
type Back = () => HTMLElement | null

interface CardProps {
  host: HostItem
  hats: HatItem[]
  hatName?: string
  onRename: (name: string, back: Back) => void
  onDefaultHat: (hat: HatItem, back: Back) => void
  onRevoke: (back: Back) => void
}

function HostCard({ host, hats, hatName, onRename, onDefaultHat, onRevoke }: CardProps) {
  const state = hostState(host)
  const [renaming, setRenaming] = useState(false)
  const [name, setName] = useState(host.name)
  const titleId = `host-${host.host_id}`
  // The rename form hides as it closes, taking focus with it: focus goes
  // back to "Rename". A revoke takes every button away: focus goes to the
  // card's title.
  const title = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null)
  const rename = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const refocus = useRef(false)
  const toRename = () => rename.current
  const toTitle = () => title.current

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!renaming && refocus.current) {
      refocus.current = false
      rename.current?.focus()
    }
  }, [renaming])

  const closeForm = () => {
    refocus.current = true
    setRenaming(false)
  }

  return (
    <li className={`card host host-${state}`} aria-labelledby={titleId}>
      <div className="card-head">
        <h2 className="card-title" id={titleId} ref={title} tabIndex={-1}>
          <Text>{host.name}</Text>
        </h2>
        <span className={`state state-${state}`}>{HOST_STATE_LABEL[state]}</span>
      </div>
      {/* One row per fact; the agents, the last doctor result and the
          host's notices join these when the host reports them. */}
      <dl className="facts">
        <dt>Platform</dt>
        <dd>
          <Text>{host.platform}</Text>
        </dd>
        <dt>hennery</dt>
        <dd>
          <Text>{host.host_version}</Text>
        </dd>
        <dt>Default hat</dt>
        <dd>{hatName !== undefined ? <Text>{hatName}</Text> : <Text className="mono">{host.default_hat_id}</Text>}</dd>
        <dt>Paired</dt>
        <dd>
          <When at={host.created_at} />
        </dd>
        <dt>Last connected</dt>
        <dd>{host.last_seen_at ? <When at={host.last_seen_at} /> : 'never'}</dd>
        {host.revoked_at && (
          <>
            <dt>Revoked on</dt>
            <dd>
              <When at={host.revoked_at} />
            </dd>
          </>
        )}
      </dl>
      {state !== 'revoked' && (
        <div className="card-actions">
          {renaming ? (
            <form
              className="inline-form"
              onSubmit={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault()
                const trimmed = name.trim()
                if (trimmed === '' || trimmed === host.name) {
                  closeForm()
                  return
                }
                // The confirmation returns focus to "Rename".
                setRenaming(false)
                onRename(trimmed, toRename)
              }}
            >
              <label className="field">
                <span className="field-label">New name</span>
                <input
                  className="text-input"
                  value={name}
                  maxLength={64}
                  onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                  autoFocus
                />
              </label>
              <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary btn-sm">
                Save
              </button>
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onClick={closeForm}>
                Cancel
              </button>
            </form>
          ) : (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm"
                ref={rename}
                onClick={() => {
                  setName(host.name)
                  setRenaming(true)
                }}
              >
                Rename
              </button>
              <label className="select-field">
                <span className="field-label">Default hat</span>
                <select
                  value={host.default_hat_id}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const hat = hats.find((h) => h.id === e.target.value)
                    if (hat) onDefaultHat(hat, toTitle)
                  }}
                >
                  {hats
                    .filter((h) => !h.purging || h.id === host.default_hat_id)
                    .map((h) => (
                      <option key={h.id} value={h.id}>
                        {visible(h.name)}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                </select>
              </label>
              <span className="spacer" />
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onClick={() => onRevoke(toTitle)}>
                Revoke
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </li>
  )
}
````

Write `web/src/screens/Mcp.tsx`:

````tsx
// MCP (frontend spec §8, gateway spec §1, §9): the gateway's connections,
// each with its state, its token (write-only) and the hosts it is mounted
// on; adding, editing and deleting one. Creating, deleting, setting a token
// and the changes that move a token's reach need a fresh step-up, which the
// client asks for when the server refuses.
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { messageOf } from '../api/errors'
import { hats as listHats, hosts as listHosts } from '../api/manage'
import { connections as listConnections, createConnection, deleteConnection, updateConnection, oauthRedirect } from '../api/mcp'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import ConfirmDialog from '../components/ConfirmDialog'
import ConnectionForm from '../components/ConnectionForm'
import Mounts from '../components/Mounts'
import TokenForm from '../components/TokenForm'
import StdioServers from '../components/StdioServers'
import OauthClientForm from '../components/OauthClientForm'
import OauthConnect from '../components/OauthConnect'
import McpStatus from '../components/McpStatus'
import { useMcpScope } from '../components/McpScope'
import type { HatItem, HostItem, McpConnectionItem, UpdateMcpConnectionRequest } from '../generated/protocol'
import { useResource } from '../hooks/useResource'
import {
  NEW_FORM,
  capped,
  changeOf,
  createRequest,
  dropsToken,
  formOf,
  kindLabel,
  serverName,
  statusLabel,
  type ConnectionForm as Form,
} from '../lib/mcp'
import { Text } from '../lib/text'
import '../manage.css'

type Pending =
  | { kind: 'delete'; connection: McpConnectionItem }
  | { kind: 'drop_token'; connection: McpConnectionItem; change: UpdateMcpConnectionRequest }

const titleId = (id: string) => `mcp-${id}`

/** The hat a new connection starts in: the default for new hosts, else the
 *  first one not being purged. */
function firstHat(hats: HatItem[]): string {
  const open = hats.filter((h) => !h.purging)
  return (open.find((h) => h.default_for_new_hosts) ?? open[0])?.id ?? ''
}

export default function Mcp({ stdio = false, oauth = false }: { stdio?: boolean; oauth?: boolean }) {
  const client = useClient()
  const scope = useMcpScope()
  const local = useResource(() => scope ? Promise.resolve([]) : listConnections(client), [!!scope])
  const list = scope ?? local
  const hats = useResource(() => listHats(client))
  const hosts = useResource(() => listHosts(client))
  const redirect = useResource(() => oauth ? oauthRedirect(client) : Promise.resolve(null), [oauth])
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false)
  const [addBusy, setAddBusy] = useState(false)
  const [addError, setAddError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  // The connection being edited, as it was when its form opened: the form
  // starts from it, and a change is what the form changed of it, so an
  // answer folded in meanwhile (a mount) cannot make a stale field look
  // changed.
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<McpConnectionItem | null>(null)
  // Refs, not state: two presses in one frame both see the first.
  const creating = useRef(false)
  const updating = useRef(false)
  const [editBusy, setEditBusy] = useState(false)
  const [editError, setEditError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<Pending | null>(null)
  // The element to focus once the list has re-rendered, by id.
  const [focus, setFocus] = useState<string | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (focus === null) return
    document.getElementById(focus)?.focus()
    setFocus(null)
  }, [focus, list.data, editing, adding])

  // As functions of the list held, so two answers landing together both
  // stay.
  const replace = (item: McpConnectionItem) =>
    list.set((prev) => (prev ?? []).map((c) => (c.id === item.id ? item : c)))
  const refresh = scope?.refresh ?? (async () => { const rows = await listConnections(client); local.set(rows); return rows })

  const create = async (form: Form, slug: string, hatId: string) => {
    if (creating.current) return
    creating.current = true
    setAddBusy(true)
    setAddError(null)
    try {
      const item = await createConnection(client, createRequest(form, slug, hatId))
      list.set((prev) => [...(prev ?? []), item])
      setAdding(false)
      setFocus(titleId(item.id))
    } catch (err) {
      setAddError(messageOf(err))
    } finally {
      creating.current = false
      setAddBusy(false)
    }
  }

  const update = async (connection: McpConnectionItem, change: UpdateMcpConnectionRequest) => {
    if (updating.current) return
    updating.current = true
    setEditBusy(true)
    setEditError(null)
    try {
      replace(await updateConnection(client, connection.id, change))
      setEditing(null)
      setFocus(`${titleId(connection.id)}-edit`)
    } catch (err) {
      setEditError(messageOf(err))
    } finally {
      updating.current = false
      setEditBusy(false)
    }
  }

  const save = (connection: McpConnectionItem, base: McpConnectionItem, form: Form) => {
    const change = changeOf(base, form)
    if (Object.keys(change).length === 0) {
      setEditing(null)
      setFocus(`${titleId(connection.id)}-edit`)
      return
    }
    // The server deletes the token with another kind or origin: say so
    // first, when there is one to lose.
    if ((connection.has_credential || connection.oauth?.client_id) && dropsToken(connection, change)) setPending({ kind: 'drop_token', connection, change })
    else void update(connection, change)
  }

  // The 204 is the server saying the token is stored (gateway spec §9): only
  // `has_credential` changes, and nothing else is read back, so a mount
  // saved meanwhile is never undone by an older copy of the list.
  const tokenSaved = (id: string) =>
    list.set((prev) => (prev ?? []).map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, has_credential: true } : c)))

  const hatName = (id: string) => hats.data?.find((h) => h.id === id)?.name

  return (
    <div className="manage">
      <header className="manage-head">
        <h1 id="mcp-heading" tabIndex={-1}>
          MCP
        </h1>
        <button
          type="button"
          id="mcp-add"
          className="btn btn-primary btn-sm"
          onClick={() => {
            setAddError(null)
            setAdding(true)
          }}
          disabled={adding || hats.data === null}
        >
          Add connection
        </button>
      </header>
      <p className="manage-subtitle">Servers your sessions receive, by hat and host.</p>
      <section className="card notice" aria-label="How sessions get these">
        <p>
          A connection reaches the agent sessions in its hat on the hosts ticked below, as an MCP server named{' '}
          <code>hennery-&lt;slug&gt;</code>. Changes apply to new and resumed sessions.
        </p>
        {hosts.data?.some(h => !h.mcp_delivery) && <p>Codex may also load your own ~/.codex MCP servers.</p>}
      </section>
      <StdioServers enabled={stdio} hosts={hosts.data} hats={hats.data} />
      {[list.error, hats.error, hosts.error, redirect.error].map(
        (shown, i) =>
          shown && (
            <p key={i} className="form-error" role="alert">
              <Text>{shown}</Text>
            </p>
          ),
      )}
      {adding && hats.data && (
        <section className="card" aria-label="Add a connection">
          <h2 className="card-title">Add a connection</h2>
          <ConnectionForm
            oauth={oauth} redirectUri={redirect.data?.redirect_uri}
            initial={NEW_FORM}
            create={{ hats: hats.data.filter((h) => !h.purging), hatId: firstHat(hats.data) }}
            submit="Create"
            busy={addBusy}
            error={addError}
            onSubmit={create}
            onCancel={() => {
              setAdding(false)
              setFocus('mcp-add')
            }}
          />
        </section>
      )}
      {list.data && list.data.length === 0 && !adding && <p className="empty">No connection yet.</p>}
      <h2 className="section-heading">Gateway connections</h2>
      <ul className="cards" aria-label="Connections">
        {(list.data ?? []).map((connection) => (
          <ConnectionCard
            oauth={oauth}
            refresh={refresh}
            waiting={scope?.waiting}
            key={connection.id}
            connection={connection}
            hatName={hatName(connection.hat_id)}
            hosts={hosts.data}
            editing={editing?.id === connection.id ? editing : null}
            editBusy={editing?.id === connection.id && editBusy}
            locked={editing?.id !== connection.id && editBusy}
            editError={editing?.id === connection.id ? editError : null}
            onEdit={() => {
              // A pending save still owns this form, its error and its focus.
              if (updating.current) return
              setEditError(null)
              setEditing(connection)
            }}
            onSave={(form, base) => save(connection, base, form)}
            onCancelEdit={() => {
              setEditing(null)
              setFocus(`${titleId(connection.id)}-edit`)
            }}
            onDelete={() => setPending({ kind: 'delete', connection })}
            onChanged={replace}
            onTokenSaved={() => tokenSaved(connection.id)}
          />
        ))}
      </ul>
      {pending?.kind === 'delete' && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title="Delete this connection?"
          confirm="Delete"
          danger
          action={async () => {
            await deleteConnection(client, pending.connection.id)
            list.set((prev) => (prev ?? []).filter((c) => c.id !== pending.connection.id))
          }}
          onClose={() => setPending(null)}
          returnFocus={() => document.getElementById('mcp-heading')}
        >
          <p>
            <Text>{pending.connection.label}</Text> goes, with {pending.connection.oauth ? 'its OAuth grant, client settings and mounts' : 'its token and its mounts'}. Sessions using{' '}
            <code>
              <Text>{serverName(pending.connection.slug)}</Text>
            </code>{' '}
            are refused from now on. A new connection needs its token set again.
          </p>
        </ConfirmDialog>
      )}
      {pending?.kind === 'drop_token' && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title="Delete the stored token?"
          confirm="Save and delete the token"
          danger
          action={async () => {
            replace(await updateConnection(client, pending.connection.id, pending.change))
            setEditing(null)
          }}
          onClose={() => setPending(null)}
          returnFocus={() => document.getElementById(`${titleId(pending.connection.id)}-edit`)}
        >
          {pending.connection.oauth && <p>This also deletes the OAuth grant and vendor client settings. Connect and enter client settings again afterwards.</p>}
          <p>
            Another server address or another kind of sign-in deletes the token stored for{' '}
            <Text>{pending.connection.label}</Text>. Set one again afterwards.
          </p>
        </ConfirmDialog>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

interface CardProps {
  oauth: boolean
  refresh: () => Promise<McpConnectionItem[]>
  waiting?: (id: string, active: boolean) => void
  connection: McpConnectionItem
  hatName?: string
  hosts: HostItem[] | null
  /** The connection as its form opened, while it is edited. */
  editing: McpConnectionItem | null
  editBusy: boolean
  locked: boolean
  editError: string | null
  onEdit: () => void
  onSave: (form: Form, base: McpConnectionItem) => void
  onCancelEdit: () => void
  onDelete: () => void
  onChanged: (connection: McpConnectionItem) => void
  onTokenSaved: () => void
}

function ConnectionCard(props: CardProps) {
  const [clientRevision, setClientRevision] = useState(0)
  const { connection: c, hatName, hosts, editing } = props
  const id = titleId(c.id)
  const tools =
    c.tool_allowlist === null ? 'Every tool' : c.tool_allowlist.length === 0 ? 'None' : c.tool_allowlist.join(', ')
  return (
    <li className="card connection" aria-labelledby={id}>
      <div className="card-head">
        <h2 className="card-title" id={id} tabIndex={-1}>
          <Text>{c.label}</Text>
        </h2>
        <span className={`state mcp-${c.status}`}>{props.oauth && c.oauth && c.status === 'needs_auth' ? 'May need sign-in' : props.oauth && c.status === 'not_connected' && c.oauth ? 'Not connected' : statusLabel(c.status)}</span>
      </div>
      {c.status_note && (
        <p className="hint">
          <Text>{capped(c.status_note)}</Text>
        </p>
      )}
      {c.status === 'needs_auth' && c.cred_kind === 'static' && (
        <p className="hint">The server refused the token: set a new one below.</p>
      )}
      {c.status === 'needs_auth' && c.cred_kind === 'none' && (
        <p className="hint">The server asks for credentials: edit the connection to send a token.</p>
      )}
      <dl className="facts">
        <dt>Agents see</dt>
        <dd>
          <Text className="mono">{serverName(c.slug)}</Text>
        </dd>
        <dt>Server URL</dt>
        <dd>
          <Text className="mono">{c.url}</Text>
        </dd>
        <dt>Hat</dt>
        <dd>{hatName !== undefined ? <Text>{hatName}</Text> : <Text className="mono">{c.hat_id}</Text>}</dd>
        <dt>Authentication</dt>
        <dd>
          {c.cred_kind === 'static' ? (
            <>
              Token in <Text className="mono">{c.static_header}</Text>
              {c.static_prefix !== '' && (
                <>
                  {' '}
                  after <Text className="mono">{JSON.stringify(c.static_prefix)}</Text>
                </>
              )}
            </>
          ) : (
            kindLabel(c.cred_kind)
          )}
        </dd>
        {c.cred_kind === 'static' && (
          <>
            <dt>Token</dt>
            <dd>{c.has_credential ? 'Set' : 'Not set yet'}</dd>
          </>
        )}
        {c.account_label && (
          <>
            <dt>Signed in as</dt>
            <dd>
              <Text>{capped(c.account_label)}</Text>
            </dd>
          </>
        )}
        <dt>Tools</dt>
        <dd>
          <Text>{tools}</Text>
        </dd>
        {c.internal_network && (
          <>
            <dt>Network</dt>
            <dd>Internal network allowed</dd>
          </>
        )}
      </dl>
      {editing ? (
        <ConnectionForm
          oauth={props.oauth} redirectUri={c.oauth?.redirect_uri}
          initial={formOf(editing)}
          submit="Save"
          busy={props.editBusy}
          error={props.editError}
          onSubmit={(form) => props.onSave(form, editing)}
          onCancel={props.onCancelEdit}
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="card-actions">
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm"
            id={`${id}-edit`}
            onClick={props.onEdit}
            aria-disabled={props.locked}
          >
            Edit
          </button>
          <span className="spacer" />
          <button type="button" className="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onClick={props.onDelete}>
            Delete
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      {c.cred_kind === 'static' && <TokenForm connection={c} hatName={hatName} onSaved={props.onTokenSaved} />}
      {props.oauth && c.cred_kind === 'oauth_client' && <OauthClientForm connection={c} onChanged={item => { setClientRevision(n => n + 1); props.onChanged(item) }} />}
      {props.oauth && (c.cred_kind === 'oauth_client' || c.cred_kind === 'oauth_dcr') && <OauthConnect key={clientRevision} connection={c} refresh={props.refresh} waiting={props.waiting} />}
      {props.oauth && <McpStatus connection={c} onChanged={props.onChanged} />}
      <Mounts connection={c} hosts={hosts} onChanged={props.onChanged} />
    </li>
  )
}
````

Write `web/src/api/auth.ts`:

````typescript
// The auth routes (kernel spec §3, §8; plan 3c) and capabilities, typed.
import type {
  AuthSessionItem,
  CapabilitiesResponse,
  PasskeyCeremony,
  PasskeyItem,
  SetupResponse,
} from '../generated/protocol'
import type { Client } from './client'
import { credentialJson, creationOptions, requestOptions } from './webauthn'

export interface SetupInput {
  token: string
  password: string
  publicUrl: string
  defaultHatName: string
}

export function setUp(client: Client, input: SetupInput): Promise<SetupResponse> {
  return client.request('POST', '/api/setup', {
    token: input.token,
    password: input.password,
    public_url: input.publicUrl,
    default_hat_name: input.defaultHatName,
  })
}

export function logIn(client: Client, password: string): Promise<void> {
  return client.request('POST', '/api/auth/login', { password })
}

export function logOut(client: Client): Promise<void> {
  return client.request('POST', '/api/auth/logout')
}

export function capabilities(client: Client): Promise<CapabilitiesResponse> {
  return client.request('GET', '/api/capabilities')
}

export function stepUpWithPassword(client: Client, password: string): Promise<void> {
  return client.request('POST', '/api/auth/step-up/password', { password }, { stepUp: false })
}

export function signedInSessions(client: Client): Promise<AuthSessionItem[]> {
  return client.request('GET', '/api/auth/sessions')
}

export function passkeys(client: Client): Promise<PasskeyItem[]> {
  return client.request('GET', '/api/auth/passkeys')
}

export function removePasskey(client: Client, id: string): Promise<void> {
  return client.request('DELETE', `/api/auth/passkeys/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`)
}

/** Start a passkey login: its options, to hand to `navigator.credentials.get`
 *  in the click that follows (a prompt needs the click's user activation). */
export function startPasskeyLogin(client: Client): Promise<PasskeyCeremony> {
  return client.request('POST', '/api/auth/passkeys/login/start')
}

export function finishPasskeyLogin(client: Client, ceremony: PasskeyCeremony, credential: Credential | null): Promise<void> {
  return client.request('POST', '/api/auth/passkeys/login/finish', {
    ceremony_id: ceremony.ceremony_id,
    credential: credentialJson(credential),
  })
}

export function startPasskeyStepUp(client: Client): Promise<PasskeyCeremony> {
  return client.request('POST', '/api/auth/step-up/passkey/start', undefined, { stepUp: false })
}

export function finishPasskeyStepUp(client: Client, ceremony: PasskeyCeremony, credential: Credential | null): Promise<void> {
  return client.request(
    'POST',
    '/api/auth/step-up/passkey/finish',
    { ceremony_id: ceremony.ceremony_id, credential: credentialJson(credential) },
    { stepUp: false },
  )
}

/** Register a passkey: start, the browser's prompt, finish. Needs a
 *  stepped-up session; a refusal opens the step-up dialog and retries. */
export async function registerPasskey(client: Client, label: string): Promise<PasskeyItem> {
  const ceremony = await client.request<PasskeyCeremony>('POST', '/api/auth/passkeys/register/start', { label })
  const credential = await navigator.credentials.create(creationOptions(ceremony.options))
  return client.request('POST', '/api/auth/passkeys/register/finish', {
    ceremony_id: ceremony.ceremony_id,
    credential: credentialJson(credential),
  })
}

/** The options of a ceremony, for `navigator.credentials.get`. */
export function getOptions(ceremony: PasskeyCeremony): CredentialRequestOptions {
  return requestOptions(ceremony.options)
}
````

Write `web/src/components/AccountSettings.tsx`:

````tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { passkeys, registerPasskey, removePasskey } from '../api/auth'
import { authDevices, revokeDevice } from '../api/device'
import { messageOf } from '../api/errors'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import { useResource } from '../hooks/useResource'
import { Text } from '../lib/text'
import { completeLogout } from '../pwa/logout'
import ConfirmDialog from './ConfirmDialog'
import SignOut from './SignOut'
import When from './When'
import type { PasskeyItem } from '../generated/protocol'

export default function AccountSettings() {
  const client = useClient()
  const keys = useResource(() => passkeys(client), [client])
  const logins = useResource(() => authDevices(client), [client])
  const [label, setLabel] = useState(''), [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null), [remove, setRemove] = useState<PasskeyItem | null>(null)
  const supported = typeof PublicKeyCredential !== 'undefined' && !!navigator.credentials?.create
  const add = async () => {
    setBusy(true); setError(null)
    try { const key = await registerPasskey(client, label.trim()); keys.set(rows => [...(rows ?? []), key]); setLabel('') }
    catch (err) { setError(messageOf(err)) } finally { setBusy(false) }
  }
  return <>
    <section className="card" aria-labelledby="sign-in-heading">
      <h2 id="sign-in-heading">Sign-in and passkeys</h2>
      <p>Sensitive changes ask you to confirm with your password or a passkey.</p>
      <p className="hint">To reset a password, use <code>hennery admin reset-password</code> on the collector. This removes passkeys and ends every login. Changing the public host name also invalidates passkeys.</p>
      {keys.error && <p role="alert">Unable to load passkeys. <Text>{keys.error}</Text> <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={keys.reload}>Retry passkeys</button></p>}
      {!keys.data && !keys.error && <p role="status">Loading passkeys…</p>}
      {keys.data?.length === 0 && <p>No passkeys registered.</p>}
      <ul className="cards" aria-label="Passkeys">{keys.data?.map(key => <li className="card" key={key.id}>
        <h3><Text>{key.label}</Text></h3>
        <dl className="facts"><dt>Created</dt><dd><When at={key.created_at} /></dd><dt>Last used</dt><dd>{key.last_used_at ? <When at={key.last_used_at} /> : 'Not used yet'}</dd></dl>
        <button className="btn btn-danger" onClick={() => setRemove(key)}>Remove <Text>{key.label}</Text></button>
      </li>)}</ul>
      <label className="field">Passkey label<input className="input" maxLength={64} value={label} onChange={e => setLabel(e.target.value)} disabled={busy} /></label>
      <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy || !supported || !keys.data} onClick={() => void add()}>Add passkey</button>
      {!supported && <p className="hint">Passkeys are unavailable in this browser. Use your password to sign in.</p>}
      {error && <p role="alert"><Text>{error}</Text></p>}
    </section>
    <section className="card" aria-labelledby="logins-heading"><h2 id="logins-heading">Signed-in devices</h2><p className="hint">Each row is a login. Revoke also removes its push registrations.</p>
      {logins.error && <p role="alert">Unable to load signed-in devices. <Text>{logins.error}</Text> <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={logins.reload}>Retry devices</button></p>}
      {!logins.data && !logins.error && <p role="status">Loading signed-in devices…</p>}
      <ul className="cards">{logins.data?.map(row => <li key={row.id} className="card"><p><Text>{row.user_agent}</Text>{row.current && ' (current login)'}</p>
        <dl className="facts"><dt>Created</dt><dd><When at={row.created_at} /></dd><dt>Last seen</dt><dd><When at={row.last_seen_at} /></dd><dt>Expires</dt><dd><When at={row.expires_at} /></dd></dl>
        <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => { setBusy(true); setError(null); void revokeDevice(client, row.id).then(async () => { if (row.current) await completeLogout(client); else logins.reload() }).catch(err => setError(messageOf(err))).finally(() => setBusy(false)) }}>Revoke {row.current ? 'current login' : 'login'}</button>
      </li>)}</ul>
    </section>
    <section className="card"><h2>This login</h2><SignOut className="btn btn-ghost" /></section>
    {remove && <ConfirmDialog title="Remove this passkey?" confirm="Remove passkey" danger onClose={() => setRemove(null)} action={async () => { await removePasskey(client, remove.id); keys.set(rows => (rows ?? []).filter(key => key.id !== remove.id)) }}>
      <p>Remove <Text>{remove.label}</Text>? You can still sign in with your password or another passkey.</p>
    </ConfirmDialog>}
  </>
}
````

````tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { saveContact } from '../api/device'
import { settings } from '../api/manage'
import { messageOf } from '../api/errors'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import type { AttachmentUsage, HatItem, HostRemovalItem } from '../generated/protocol'
import { useResource } from '../hooks/useResource'
import { Text } from '../lib/text'
import { Link } from '../router'

export default function CollectorSettings({ push, sessions, hats }: { push: boolean; sessions: boolean; hats: HatItem[] | null }) {
  const client = useClient()
  const config = useResource(() => settings(client), [client])
  const usage = useResource(() => sessions ? client.request<AttachmentUsage>('GET', '/api/settings/attachments') : Promise.resolve(null), [client, sessions])
  const removals = useResource(() => sessions ? client.request<HostRemovalItem[]>('GET', '/api/settings/host-removals') : Promise.resolve(null), [client, sessions])
  const [contact, setContact] = useState<string | null>(null), [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null), [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null)
  return <section className="card" aria-labelledby="collector-heading"><h2 id="collector-heading">Collector</h2>
    {config.error && <p role="alert">Unable to load collector information. <Text>{config.error}</Text> <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={config.reload}>Retry collector</button></p>}
    {!config.data && !config.error && <p role="status">Loading collector information…</p>}
    <dl className="facts"><dt>Public URL</dt><dd>{config.data ? <Text>{config.data.public_url}</Text> : 'Not loaded'}</dd>
      {sessions && <><dt>Default for new hosts</dt><dd>{hats ? <Text>{hats.find(hat => hat.default_for_new_hosts)?.name ?? 'None reported'}</Text> : 'Not loaded'}</dd></>}
      {usage.data && <><dt>Attachments</dt><dd>{usage.data.count} images · {usage.data.bytes.toLocaleString()} bytes</dd></>}
    </dl>
    <p className="hint">The public URL is managed on the collector. <code>hennery admin reset-public-url</code> ends logins and push registrations; a changed host name removes passkeys.</p>
    {sessions && <p><Link to="/hats">Manage hats</Link> · <Link to="/hosts">Per-host defaults</Link></p>}
    {push && config.data && <div className="collector-contact"><p className="hint">The push contact lets a notification service contact the collector operator.</p>
      <label className="field">Push contact<input className="input" disabled={busy} value={contact ?? config.data.contact ?? ''} onChange={e => setContact(e.target.value)} /></label>
      <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => { setBusy(true); setError(null); setNotice(null); void saveContact(client, contact ?? config.data!.contact ?? '').then(value => { setContact(value.contact ?? ''); setNotice('Contact saved.') }).catch(err => setError(messageOf(err))).finally(() => setBusy(false)) }}>Save contact</button>
      {error && <p role="alert"><Text>{error}</Text></p>}{notice && <p role="status">{notice}</p>}
    </div>}
    {sessions && <><h3>Host transcript cleanup</h3>
      {usage.error && <p role="alert">Attachment usage could not be loaded. <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={usage.reload}>Retry usage</button></p>}
      {removals.error && <p role="alert">Cleanup status could not be loaded. <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={removals.reload}>Retry cleanup</button></p>}
      {removals.data?.length === 0 && <p>No host cleanup records reported.</p>}
      {!removals.data && !removals.error && <p role="status">Loading cleanup status…</p>}
      <ul className="cards">{removals.data?.map(row => <li className="card" key={row.id}><p><Text>{row.agent}</Text>: {row.state === 'pending' ? 'Waiting for the host to retry' : row.last_result?.state === 'removed' ? 'Transcript removed' : 'Cleanup completed with remaining copies'}</p>
        <p>{row.attempts} attempts · {row.last_result?.remaining.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.count, 0) ?? 0} entries reported remaining</p>
      </li>)}</ul>
      <p className="hint">Agents run as the host’s OS user. Use a separate OS user for stronger isolation. Cleanup is best effort; copies outside the reported transcript may remain.</p>
    </>}
  </section>
}
````

Write `web/src/components/NotificationSettings.tsx`:

````tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { policies as listPolicies, pushDevices, removePush, savePolicy, vapid } from '../api/device'
import { hats as listHats } from '../api/manage'
import { ApiFailure, messageOf } from '../api/errors'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import { useResource } from '../hooks/useResource'
import type { PushPolicyItem, PushSubscriptionItem } from '../generated/protocol'
import { Text } from '../lib/text'
import { BrowserPush, classifyPush, type PushState } from '../pwa/device'
import { pushJournal, type PushRecord } from '../pwa/journal'
import { registerWorker, usePwa } from '../pwa/lifecycle'
import { vapidBytes } from '../pwa/subscription'
import '../manage.css'

const COPY: Record<PushState, string> = {
  unsupported: 'This browser does not support notifications. Install the app where supported.',
  insecure: 'Notifications require a secure HTTPS connection.',
  blocked: 'Notifications are blocked by browser or OS settings. Change those settings to enable them.',
  off: 'Notifications are off on this browser.',
  unconfirmed: 'Browser registration is unconfirmed. Enable to register or repair it.',
  reenable: 'Enable again: the device was removed, its subscription changed, or collector keys changed.',
  signed_out: 'This push registration belongs to an expired login. Explicitly enable to bind it to this login.',
  enabled: 'Notifications are enabled on this browser.',
}
const supported = () => 'serviceWorker' in navigator && !!navigator.locks && typeof PushManager !== 'undefined' && typeof Notification !== 'undefined'
const hintDismissed = () => { try { return !!sessionStorage.getItem('hennery.install-hint-dismissed') } catch { return false } }

export default function NotificationSettings({ push, sessions }: { push: boolean; sessions: boolean }) {
  const client = useClient(), pwa = usePwa()
  const [devices, setDevices] = useState<PushSubscriptionItem[]>([])
  const [key, setKey] = useState('')
  const privacy = useResource(async () => { if (!sessions) return { hats: [], policies: [] }; const [hats, policies] = await Promise.all([listHats(client), listPolicies(client)]); return { hats, policies } }, [client, sessions])
  const [inspection, setInspection] = useState<{ subscription: PushSubscription | null; record: PushRecord; matches: boolean } | null>(null)
  const [checked, setChecked] = useState<number | null>(null), [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null), [busy, setBusy] = useState(false), [prepared, setPrepared] = useState(false)
  const [label, setLabel] = useState(''), [hint, setHint] = useState(!hintDismissed())
  const device = useMemo(() => pwa.registration && key ? new BrowserPush(client, pwa.registration, pushJournal(), key) : null, [client, pwa.registration, key])

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      if (push) {
        const observed = pwa.registration && supported() ? await pushJournal().read() : null
        const [rows, loadedKey] = await Promise.all([pushDevices(client), vapid(client)])
        vapidBytes(loadedKey.public_key)
        if (pwa.registration && supported()) {
          const inspected = await new BrowserPush(client, pwa.registration, pushJournal(), loadedKey.public_key).reconcile(rows, fetch, observed?.generation)
          if (inspected.stale) throw new Error('Another window changed notification settings; refresh to check them.')
          setInspection(inspected)
        }
        setDevices(rows); setKey(loadedKey.public_key)
      }
      setChecked(Date.now()); setError(null)
    } catch (err) { setError('Unable to check device settings: ' + messageOf(err)) }
  }, [client, push, sessions, pwa.registration])
  useEffect(() => { void refresh() }, [refresh])

  const run = async (action: () => Promise<void>) => {
    setBusy(true); setError(null); setNotice(null)
    try { await action() } catch (err) { setError(messageOf(err)) } finally { setBusy(false) }
  }
  const state = classifyPush({ supported: supported(), secure: globalThis.isSecureContext === true,
    permission: typeof Notification === 'undefined' ? 'default' : Notification.permission,
    subscribed: !!inspection?.subscription, matches: inspection?.matches ?? true,
    record: inspection?.record ?? { generation: 0, enabled: false }, row: devices.find((r) => r.id === inspection?.record.id) })
  const canEnable = !['unsupported', 'insecure', 'blocked'].includes(state) && !!device
  const iosTab = /iPad|iPhone|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgent) && !window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches && !(navigator as Navigator & { standalone?: boolean }).standalone
  const expires = inspection?.subscription?.expirationTime
  const renewable = state === 'enabled' && expires != null && expires <= Date.now() + 86400000

  return <>
    {error && <p role="alert" className="form-error"><Text>{error}</Text>{checked && <> Last checked {new Date(checked).toLocaleString()}.</>}</p>}
    {notice && <p role="status">{notice}</p>}
    <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(refresh)}>Refresh settings</button>
    {push && <section className="card" aria-labelledby="browser-push-heading">
      <h2 id="browser-push-heading">Notifications on this browser</h2>
      {hint && <div><p>Install hennery for convenient access. Enable notifications only when you want them.</p>
        <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => { try { sessionStorage.setItem('hennery.install-hint-dismissed', '1') } catch { /* Nonessential preference. */ } setHint(false) }}>Dismiss install hint</button></div>}
      {iosTab && <p>Use your browser’s Share or menu control, Add to Home Screen, then launch that app and sign in there if needed before enabling notifications.</p>}
      <p role="status">{COPY[state]}</p>
      {!pwa.registration && !pwa.error && supported() && globalThis.isSecureContext === true && <p role="status">Preparing the offline shell…</p>}
      {pwa.error && <p role="alert">{pwa.error} <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => void registerWorker()}>Retry worker setup</button></p>}
      <label className="field">Device label (optional)<input className="input" value={label} maxLength={64} onChange={(e) => setLabel(e.target.value)} /></label>
      <div className="card-actions">
        {canEnable && (prepared ? <button className="btn btn-primary" disabled={busy} onClick={() => {
          // The permission API is invoked in this tap before ANY await.
          const permission = Notification.permission === 'granted' ? Promise.resolve('granted') : Notification.requestPermission()
          void run(async () => {
            if (await permission !== 'granted') { setPrepared(false); setNotice('Notifications were not enabled. Check browser or OS settings.'); return }
            await device!.enable(label.trim() || undefined); setPrepared(false); await refresh()
          })
        }}>Enable notifications</button> : <button className="btn btn-primary" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(async () => { await client.confirm(); setPrepared(true) })}>Prepare notifications</button>)}
        <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy || !device} onClick={() => void run(async () => {
          const result = await device!.disable(); setPrepared(false)
          setNotice(result.server && result.browser ? 'Notifications disabled.' : !result.server && !result.browser ? 'Collector removal and browser cleanup are unconfirmed. Retry Disable.' : !result.server ? 'Browser unsubscribed; collector removal is unconfirmed. Retry Disable.' : 'Collector registration removed; browser cleanup is unconfirmed. Retry Disable.')
          await refresh()
        })}>Disable notifications</button>
        {renewable && <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(async () => { await device!.renew(devices); await refresh() })}>Renew subscription</button>}
      </div>
      <p className="hint">The icon does not guarantee offline storage. An offline first visit needs a successful installation first. Receiving a push does not make the collector reachable.</p>
    </section>}
    {push && <section className="card"><h2>Push devices</h2><p className="hint">These are registrations, not unique physical devices. Removing one stops delivery; it does not sign that browser out.</p>
      <ul className="cards">{devices.map((row) => <li className="card" key={row.id}>
        <h3><Text>{row.device_label}</Text>{row.id === inspection?.record.id && ' (this registration)'}</h3>
        <dl className="facts"><dt>Endpoint host</dt><dd><Text>{row.endpoint_host}</Text></dd><dt>Created</dt><dd>{row.created_at}</dd>
          <dt>Last service acceptance</dt><dd>{row.last_success_at ?? 'None reported'}</dd><dt>Delivery error</dt><dd><Text>{row.last_error ?? 'None reported'}</Text></dd></dl>
        {row.signed_out && <p>Signed out or expired; this registration can still receive push.</p>}
        <p className="hint">Service acceptance does not prove the OS displayed or you read a notification.</p>
        <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(async () => {
          if (row.id === inspection?.record.id && device) {
            const result = await device.disable()
            if (!result.server || !result.browser) setNotice('Removal or browser cleanup is unconfirmed. Retry Disable.')
          } else await removePush(client, { id: row.id })
          await refresh()
        })}>Remove <Text>{row.device_label}</Text></button>
      </li>)}</ul>
    </section>}
    {sessions && <section className="card" aria-labelledby="privacy-heading"><h2 id="privacy-heading">Notification privacy by hat</h2><p>These settings apply to every push device for this hat. Question details are agent text and may contain secrets. A generic title with details still reveals that text.</p>
      {privacy.error && <p role="alert">Unable to load notification privacy. <Text>{privacy.error}</Text> <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={privacy.reload}>Retry privacy</button></p>}
      {!privacy.data && !privacy.error && <p role="status">Loading notification privacy…</p>}
      {(privacy.data?.policies ?? []).map(policy => <Policy key={policy.hat_id} policy={policy} name={privacy.data?.hats.find(h => h.id === policy.hat_id)?.name ?? 'Unavailable hat'} onRemove={privacy.reload} />)}
    </section>}
  </>

}

function Policy({ policy, name, onRemove }: { policy: PushPolicyItem; name: string; onRemove: () => void }) {
  const client = useClient(), [value, setValue] = useState(policy), [busy, setBusy] = useState(false), [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  return <fieldset className="card" disabled={busy}><legend><Text>{name}</Text></legend>
    {(['muted', 'generic_title', 'details'] as const).map((key, i) => <label className="policy-control" key={key}>
      <input type="checkbox" checked={value[key]} onChange={(e) => setValue({ ...value, [key]: e.target.checked })} />{['Mute', 'Use a generic title', 'Include question details'][i]}
    </label>)}
    <p>Preview: {value.muted ? 'No notification' : value.generic_title ? 'hennery needs your answer' : 'Example project needs your answer'}{!value.muted && value.details && ' — Example question text (may contain secrets)'}</p>
    {error && <p role="alert"><Text>{error}</Text></p>}
    <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => {
      setBusy(true); setError(null)
      const { muted, details, generic_title } = value
      void savePolicy(client, policy.hat_id, { muted, details, generic_title }).then(setValue, (err: unknown) => {
        if (err instanceof ApiFailure && err.status === 404) onRemove()
        else setError(messageOf(err))
      }).finally(() => setBusy(false))
    }}>Save policy</button>
  </fieldset>
}
````

    const agent = screen.getByText('<script>bad</script>', { exact: true })
    expect(agent.tagName).toBe('BDI')
    expect(document.querySelector('script')).toBeNull()
  })
})

````tsx
import { hats as listHats } from '../api/manage'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import AccountSettings from '../components/AccountSettings'
import CollectorSettings from '../components/CollectorSettings'
import NotificationSettings from '../components/NotificationSettings'
import { AppearanceOptions } from '../components/ui'
import { useResource } from '../hooks/useResource'
import '../manage.css'

export default function Settings({ push, sessions }: { push: boolean; sessions: boolean }) {
  const client = useClient()
  const hats = useResource(() => sessions ? listHats(client) : Promise.resolve([]), [client, sessions])
  return <div className="manage device-settings">
    <header className="manage-head"><h1>Settings</h1></header>
    <p className="manage-subtitle">Your account, browser and collector preferences.</p>
    <section className="card"><h2>Appearance</h2><p className="hint">System follows this device. The selected hat supplies the accent.</p><AppearanceOptions /></section>
    <AccountSettings />
    {push && <NotificationSettings push={push} sessions={sessions} />}
    {hats.error && <p role="alert">Unable to load hats. <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={hats.reload}>Retry hats</button></p>}
    <CollectorSettings push={push} sessions={sessions} hats={hats.data} />
  </div>
}
````
