Write `web/e2e/management-captures.ts`:

````typescript
import { expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test'
import { appendFileSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { contrast } from '../src/lib/accent'
/** Optional visual ledger; checks always run against the embedded UI. */
export async function captureManagement(page: Page, state: string, reference: string) {
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= innerWidth)).toBe(true)
  const directory = process.env.HENNERY_UI_R5_CAPTURE_DIR
  if (directory) mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true })
  const viewport = page.viewportSize()!
  const main = page.locator('.main')
  const scrollTop = await main.evaluate(element => element.scrollTop)
  for (const theme of ['light', 'dark'] as const) {
    await main.evaluate((element, top) => { element.scrollTop = top }, scrollTop)
    await page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme: theme, reducedMotion: 'reduce' })
    await expect(page.locator('html')).toHaveAttribute('data-theme', theme)
    await page.evaluate(() => document.fonts.ready)
    const controls = await page.locator('.manage .input, .manage .text-input, .manage select, .manage textarea').evaluateAll(elements => elements.filter(element => element.getClientRects().length > 0).map(element => {
      const style = getComputedStyle(element)
      return { boundary: style.borderTopColor, background: style.backgroundColor }
    }))
    const hex = (rgb: string) => '#' + (rgb.match(/\d+/g) ?? []).slice(0, 3).map(value => Number(value).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('')
    for (const control of controls) expect(contrast(hex(control.boundary), hex(control.background))).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3)
    if (!directory) continue
    const file = `${state}-${viewport.width}-${theme}.png`
    await page.screenshot({ path: join(directory, file), fullPage: true })
    appendFileSync(join(directory, 'captures.tsv'), `${file}\t${viewport.width}x${viewport.height}\t${page.url().replace(new URL(page.url()).origin, '')}\t${reference}\n`)
    // Management scrolls inside the shell. Record the top and each section,
    // rather than mistaking a fullPage image of a scrolled shell for all UI.
    if (await page.locator('.overlay').count() === 0 && ['settings', 'settings-passkeys', 'hats', 'hosts'].includes(state)) {
      const headings = page.locator('.manage > header, .manage > section > h2, .manage > .cards > li > .card-head > h2')
      for (let index = 0; index < await headings.count(); index++) {
        await headings.nth(index).scrollIntoViewIfNeeded()
        const section = `${state}-section-${index}-${viewport.width}-${theme}.png`
        await page.screenshot({ path: join(directory, section) })
        appendFileSync(join(directory, 'captures.tsv'), `${section}\t${viewport.width}x${viewport.height}\t${page.url().replace(new URL(page.url()).origin, '')}\t${reference}\n`)
      }
    }
  }
  await main.evaluate((element, top) => { element.scrollTop = top }, scrollTop)
  await page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme: 'light' })
}
````

Write `web/e2e/mcp-delivery.spec.ts`:

````typescript
import { captureManagement } from './management-captures'
// Real dispatch receipts, not MCP tool execution: the Codex-named fake
// records session/new and session/load under its own scratch directory.
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test'
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, realpathSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs'
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { startCollector, type Collector } from './collector'
import { fakeAgent, testHost, type TestHost } from './host'
import { scratchEnv } from './spawn'

const PASSWORD = 'correct horse battery staple'
const ENV_VALUE = 'e2e-environment-value-0123456789'
async function stepUp(page: Page) {
  const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'This needs a fresh confirmation', exact: true })
  await expect(dialog).toBeVisible()
  await dialog.getByLabel('Your password', { exact: true }).fill(PASSWORD)
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm', exact: true }).click()
  await expect(dialog).toBeHidden()
}

for (const width of [1280, 390]) test.describe(`MCP delivery at ${width} px`, () => {
  test.describe.configure({ mode: 'serial' })
  let collector: Collector, host: TestHost, page: Page
  let project: string, receipt: string, sessionUrl: string, hostId: string, hatId: string, sentinel: string
  let runnerLog: string | undefined
  const violations: string[] = []
  const records = () => existsSync(receipt) ? readFileSync(receipt, 'utf8').trim().split('\n').map(line => JSON.parse(line)) : []
  const local = () => page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Local MCP servers', exact: true })
  const report = () => page.getByRole('region', { name: 'MCP delivery', exact: true })
  test.beforeAll(async ({ browser }) => {
    sentinel = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'hennery-e2e-sentinel-'))
    runnerLog = process.env.HENNERY_LOG_DIR; process.env.HENNERY_LOG_DIR = sentinel
    expect(scratchEnv(sentinel).HENNERY_LOG_DIR).toBeUndefined()
    collector = await startCollector()
    // The log is scratch too, and never printed: it can contain env values.
    const agentScratch = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'hennery-e2e-receipts-'))
    receipt = join(agentScratch, 'session.jsonl')
    host = testHost(fakeAgent('codex', { chunks: ['Done.'], session_log: receipt }))
    project = join(realpathSync(host.dir), 'notes'); mkdirSync(project)
    const context = await browser.newContext({ baseURL: collector.origin, viewport: { width, height: 844 } })
    page = await context.newPage()
    await page.addInitScript(() => document.addEventListener('securitypolicyviolation', e => {
      const seen = ((window as unknown as { __csp?: string[] }).__csp ??= [])
      seen.push(`${e.violatedDirective} ${e.blockedURI}`)
    }))
    page.on('console', m => { if (m.text().includes('Content Security Policy')) violations.push(m.text()) })
    const setup = await page.request.post('/api/setup', { headers: { Origin: collector.origin }, data: { token: new URL(collector.setupLink).hash.slice(1), password: PASSWORD, public_url: collector.origin } })
    expect(setup.status()).toBe(201)
  })
  test.afterAll(async () => {
    try { await page?.context().close() } finally {
      try { await host?.stop() } finally {
        try { await collector?.stop() } finally {
          if (runnerLog === undefined) delete process.env.HENNERY_LOG_DIR; else process.env.HENNERY_LOG_DIR = runnerLog
          if (receipt) rmSync(join(receipt, '..'), { recursive: true, force: true })
          if (sentinel) rmSync(sentinel, { recursive: true, force: true })
        }
      }
    }
  })
  test('pairs a real host running a Codex-named fake', async () => {
    await page.goto('/hosts'); await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add host', exact: true }).click()
    const command = (await page.getByLabel('Pairing command', { exact: true }).textContent())!
    expect(await host.join(command, ['--name', 'e2e host', '--no-runtime'])).toBe(0)
    await expect(page.getByText('Paired: e2e host', { exact: true })).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15_000 })
    host.run()
    await expect(async () => {
      const response = await page.request.get('/api/hosts'); expect(response.status()).toBe(200)
      const hosts = await response.json(); expect(hosts[0].connected).toBe(true)
      hostId = hosts[0].host_id; hatId = hosts[0].default_hat_id
    }).toPass({ timeout: 20_000 })
  })
  test('saves a full local set through step-up, reads no values back and starts with its actual delivery', async () => {
    await page.goto('/mcp'); await expect(local()).toBeVisible()
    // The existing connection form creates and mounts the HTTP entry.
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add connection', exact: true }).click()
    const connection = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Add a connection', exact: true })
    await connection.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Name', exact: true }).fill('Docs')
    await connection.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Slug', exact: true }).fill('docs')
    await connection.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Server URL', exact: true }).fill('https://mcp.example.com/mcp')
    await connection.getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Authentication', exact: true }).selectOption('none')
    await connection.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create', exact: true }).click()
    const tick = page.getByRole('listitem', { name: 'Docs', exact: true }).getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'e2e host', exact: true })
    await tick.click(); await expect(tick).toBeChecked()
    await local().getByRole('button', { name: 'Add local server', exact: true }).click()
    await local().getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Server name', exact: true }).fill('local')
    await local().getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Command', exact: true }).fill('tool')
    await local().getByRole('button', { name: 'Add environment variable', exact: true }).click()
    await local().getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Environment name', exact: true }).fill('TOKEN')
    await local().getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Value for TOKEN', exact: true }).fill(ENV_VALUE)
    await captureManagement(page, 'mcp-stdio', 'Desktop mcp / local stdio')
    await local().getByRole('button', { name: 'Review local servers', exact: true }).click()
    await page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Save these local MCP servers?', exact: true }).getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(local().getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Value for TOKEN', exact: true })).toHaveValue('')
    await local().getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Value for TOKEN', exact: true }).fill(ENV_VALUE)
    let refused = 0
    await page.route('**/api/mcp/stdio-servers?*', async route => {
      if (route.request().method() === 'PUT' && refused === 0) { refused++; await route.fulfill({ status: 403, contentType: 'application/json', body: JSON.stringify({ code: 'step_up_required' }) }) }
      else await route.continue()
    })
    await local().getByRole('button', { name: 'Review local servers', exact: true }).click()
    await page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Save these local MCP servers?', exact: true }).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save local servers', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(local().getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Value for TOKEN', exact: true })).toHaveValue('')
    await stepUp(page)
    await expect(local().getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Keep stored value for TOKEN', exact: true })).toBeChecked()
    expect(refused).toBe(1); await page.unroute('**/api/mcp/stdio-servers?*')
    const listed = await page.request.get(`/api/mcp/stdio-servers?host_id=${hostId}&hat_id=${hatId}`)
    expect(listed.status()).toBe(200)
    const body = await listed.text(); expect(body.includes(ENV_VALUE)).toBe(false)
    expect(JSON.parse(body).servers[0].env).toEqual([{ name: 'TOKEN', has_value: true }])
    expect(await page.locator('textarea').evaluateAll((fields, value) => fields.some(field => field instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement && field.value.includes(value)), ENV_VALUE)).toBe(false); expect(page.url().includes(ENV_VALUE)).toBe(false)
    expect(await page.evaluate(value => document.body.innerText.includes(value), ENV_VALUE)).toBe(false)
    expect(await page.evaluate(value => JSON.stringify({ ...localStorage, ...sessionStorage }).includes(value), ENV_VALUE)).toBe(false)
    if (width === 390) expect((await local().getByRole('button', { name: 'Review local servers', exact: true }).boundingBox())!.height).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(44)
    await page.goto('/new')
    const form = page.getByRole('form', { name: 'New session', exact: true }); await form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Codex', exact: true }).click()
    await form.getByRole('combobox').fill(project)
    await expect(form.getByRole('region', { name: 'MCP prediction', exact: true })).toContainText('not isolated per hat')
    await form.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'First prompt optional', exact: true }).fill('Show delivery.')
    await form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start session', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/sessions\/[^/?#]+$/, { timeout: 45_000 }); sessionUrl = page.url()
    await expect(report()).toContainText('At the last start/resume:')
    await expect(report().getByText('2 MCP servers.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await expect(report()).toContainText('Codex also loads your own ~/.codex MCP servers.')
    expect(records()[0].method).toBe('session/new')
    expect(records()[0].params.mcpServers.map((s: { name: string }) => s.name).sort()).toEqual(['hennery-docs', 'hennery-local'])
    expect(JSON.stringify(records()[0].params.mcpServers).includes(ENV_VALUE)).toBe(true)
    await page.reload(); await expect(report().getByText('2 MCP servers.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
  })
  test('keeps the last-start snapshot until resume, and delivers none for a different hat', async () => {
    await page.goto('/mcp'); await local().getByRole('button', { name: 'Add local server', exact: true }).click()
    await local().getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Server name', exact: true }).nth(1).fill('second')
    await local().getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Command', exact: true }).nth(1).fill('runner')
    await local().getByRole('button', { name: 'Review local servers', exact: true }).click()
    await page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Save these local MCP servers?', exact: true }).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save local servers', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(page.getByRole('dialog')).toHaveCount(0)
    await page.goto(sessionUrl); await expect(report().getByText('2 MCP servers.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.locator('.session-head .badge')).toHaveText('Idle')
    await host.crash(); host.run()
    await expect(page.locator('.session-head .badge')).toHaveText('Parked', { timeout: 20_000 })
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Resume', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(report()).toContainText('3 MCP servers.', { timeout: 20_000 })
    expect(records().at(-1).method).toBe('session/load'); expect(records().at(-1).params.mcpServers.length).toBe(3)
    const made = await page.request.post('/api/hats', { headers: { Origin: collector.origin }, data: { name: 'Other hat' } }); expect(made.status()).toBe(201)
    const other = await made.json()
    const path = join(project, 'other'); mkdirSync(path)
    const rules = await page.request.put(`/api/hosts/${hostId}/path-rules`, { headers: { Origin: collector.origin }, data: { rules: [{ prefix: path, hat_id: other.id }] } }); expect(rules.status()).toBe(200)
    await page.goto('/new')
    const form = page.getByRole('form', { name: 'New session', exact: true }); await form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Codex', exact: true }).click(); await form.getByRole('combobox').fill(path)
    await expect(form.getByRole('region', { name: 'MCP prediction', exact: true })).toContainText('this hat is not the host’s default')
    await form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start session', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(report()).toContainText('this hat is not the host’s default', { timeout: 45_000 })
    await expect(report()).toContainText('0 MCP servers.')
    expect(records().at(-1).params.mcpServers).toEqual([])
  })
  test('obeys CSP and leaves the runner’s log directory untouched', async () => {
    expect(violations).toEqual([])
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as unknown as { __csp?: string[] }).__csp ?? [])).toEqual([])
    expect(readdirSync(sentinel)).toEqual([])
  })
})
````

Write `web/e2e/mcp-oauth.spec.ts`:

````typescript
import { captureManagement } from './management-captures'
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test'
import { startCollector, type Collector } from './collector'
import { startVendor, type Vendor } from './oauth-vendor'
import { testHost, type TestHost } from './host'

const PASSWORD = 'correct horse battery staple'
const SECRET = 'e2e-vendor-secret-canary'
for (const width of [1280, 390]) test.describe(`OAuth at ${width} px`, () => {
  test.describe.configure({ mode: 'serial' })
  let collector: Collector, vendor: Vendor, page: Page
  let host: TestHost, mountedHost: string
  const card = (name = 'OAuth docs') => page.getByRole('listitem', { name, exact: true })
  const listed = async (slug = 'oauth-docs') => {
    const response = await page.request.get('/api/mcp/connections'); expect(response.status()).toBe(200)
    const text = await response.text(); expect(text).not.toContain(SECRET)
    return JSON.parse(text).find((c: { slug: string }) => c.slug === slug)
  }
  async function add(name: string, slug: string, kind = 'oauth_dcr') {
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add connection', exact: true }).click()
    const form = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Add a connection', exact: true })
    await form.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Name', exact: true }).fill(name)
    await form.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Slug', exact: true }).fill(slug)
    await form.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Server URL', exact: true }).fill(vendor.mcpUrl)
    await form.getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Authentication', exact: true }).selectOption(kind)
    await form.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Internal network', exact: true }).check()
    await form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(card(name)).toBeVisible()
  }
  async function popup(name = 'OAuth docs', button = 'Reconnect') {
    const opened = page.context().waitForEvent('page')
    await card(name).getByRole('button', { name: button, exact: true }).click()
    const vendorPage = await opened
    await expect(vendorPage.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Vendor consent', exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    expect(await vendorPage.evaluate(() => window.opener === null)).toBe(true)
    expect(vendor.counts.pathReferrers).toBe(0)
    return vendorPage
  }
  async function allow(vendorPage: Page, name = 'OAuth docs') {
    const returned = vendorPage.waitForResponse(r => new URL(r.url()).pathname === '/api/mcp/oauth/callback')
    await vendorPage.getByRole('link', { name: 'Allow', exact: true }).click()
    const response = await returned; expect(response.status()).toBe(200)
    const headers = response.headers()
    expect(headers['content-security-policy']).toContain("script-src 'self'")
    expect(headers['cache-control']).toBe('no-store'); expect(headers['referrer-policy']).toBe('no-referrer')
    expect(headers['x-content-type-options']).toBe('nosniff')
    const body = await response.text(); expect(body).not.toContain(SECRET)
    const code = new URL(response.url()).searchParams.get('code')!
    expect(body).not.toContain(code); expect(body).toContain('/api/mcp/oauth/callback.js')
    await vendorPage.waitForURL(`${collector.origin}/api/mcp/oauth/callback`)
    await expect(card(name).getByText('Working', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await expect(async () => expect(vendorPage.isClosed()).toBe(true)).toPass()
  }
  test.beforeAll(async ({ browser }) => {
    vendor = await startVendor(); collector = await startCollector(); host = testHost()
    const context = await browser.newContext({ baseURL: collector.origin, viewport: { width, height: 844 } })
    page = await context.newPage()
    const setup = await page.request.post('/api/setup', { headers: { Origin: collector.origin }, data: { token: new URL(collector.setupLink).hash.slice(1), password: PASSWORD, public_url: collector.origin } })
    expect(setup.status()).toBe(201); await page.goto('/mcp')
  })
  test.afterAll(async () => { try { await page?.context().close() } finally { try { await host?.stop() } finally { try { await collector?.stop() } finally { await vendor?.stop() } } } })
  test('creates DCR and completes real consent/cookie/PKCE exchange', async () => {
    await add('OAuth docs', 'oauth-docs')
    expect((await listed()).has_credential).toBe(false)
    await allow(await popup('OAuth docs', 'Connect'))
    await captureManagement(page, 'mcp-oauth', 'Desktop mcp / OAuth')
    expect((await listed()).has_credential).toBe(true); expect(vendor.counts.registrations).toBe(1); expect(vendor.counts.exchanges).toBe(1)
    const stored = await page.evaluate(() => JSON.stringify({ ...localStorage, ...sessionStorage }))
    expect(stored).not.toContain(vendor.origin); expect(stored).not.toContain('/authorize')
    if (width === 390) expect((await card().getByRole('button', { name: 'Reconnect', exact: true }).boundingBox())!.height).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(44)
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= innerWidth)).toBe(true)
  })
  test('mounts the OAuth grant on a real paired host before reconnecting', async () => {
    await page.goto('/hosts'); await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add host', exact: true }).click()
    const command = (await page.getByLabel('Pairing command', { exact: true }).textContent())!
    expect(await host.join(command, ['--name', 'OAuth test host', '--no-runtime'])).toBe(0)
    await expect(page.getByText('Paired: OAuth test host', { exact: true })).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 })
    await page.goto('/mcp')
    const mounted = card().getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'OAuth test host', exact: true })
    await mounted.click(); await expect(mounted).toBeChecked()
    const mounts = (await listed()).mounts; expect(mounts).toHaveLength(1); mountedHost = mounts[0]
  })
  test('denial is inert text and preserves a working grant', async () => {
    vendor.config.deny = true
    const p = await popup(); const returned = p.waitForResponse(r => new URL(r.url()).pathname === '/api/mcp/oauth/callback')
    await p.getByRole('link', { name: 'Deny', exact: true }).click(); const response = await returned
    expect(response.status()).toBe(400); expect(response.headers()['content-security-policy']).toBeTruthy()
    await expect(p.getByText('The vendor said: access_denied: <script>denied</script>', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    expect(await p.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('script').length)).toBe(1)
    await expect(card().getByRole('region', { name: 'Gateway report', exact: true }).getByText('The vendor said: access_denied: <script>denied</script>', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    // The handler closes its own page before Chromium may acknowledge the click.
    // Require that close event, and preserve failures unrelated to target closure.
    const closed = p.waitForEvent('close')
    await Promise.all([closed, p.getByRole('button', { name: 'Close', exact: true }).click().catch((error: unknown) => {
      if (!(error instanceof Error) || !error.message.startsWith('locator.click: Target page, context or browser has been closed')) throw error
    })])
    expect((await listed()).status).toBe('ok'); expect((await listed()).has_credential).toBe(true)
    expect((await listed()).mounts).toEqual([mountedHost])
    vendor.config.deny = false
  })
  test('COOP severs the handle without ending consent', async () => {
    vendor.config.coop = true; const p = await popup()
    await expect(card().getByText('Waiting for sign-in. Return here after consent.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await allow(p); vendor.config.coop = false
  })
  test('blocked popup uses a noopener/noreferrer link', async () => {
    await page.evaluate(() => { const open = window.open; window.open = () => null; (window as unknown as { restoreOpen: () => void }).restoreOpen = () => { window.open = open } })
    await card().getByRole('button', { name: 'Reconnect', exact: true }).click()
    const link = card().getByRole('link', { name: 'Open vendor sign-in', exact: true })
    await expect(link).toHaveAttribute('rel', 'noopener noreferrer')
    const opened = page.context().waitForEvent('page'); await link.click(); const p = await opened
    await expect(p.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Vendor consent', exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    expect(await p.evaluate(() => window.opener === null)).toBe(true)
    await page.evaluate(() => (window as unknown as { restoreOpen: () => void }).restoreOpen())
    await allow(p)
  })
  test('authorize closes before step-up and waits for a fresh click', async () => {
    let requests = 0
    await page.route('**/api/mcp/connections/*/authorize', async route => {
      requests++; if (requests === 1) await route.fulfill({ status: 403, contentType: 'application/json', body: JSON.stringify({ code: 'step_up_required' }) }); else await route.continue()
    })
    const opened = page.context().waitForEvent('page'); await card().getByRole('button', { name: 'Reconnect', exact: true }).click(); const blank = await opened
    const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'This needs a fresh confirmation', exact: true }); await expect(dialog).toBeVisible()
    expect(blank.isClosed()).toBe(true)
    await dialog.getByLabel('Your password', { exact: true }).fill(PASSWORD); await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(card().getByText('Confirmed. Click Reconnect again to open sign-in.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible(); expect(requests).toBe(1)
    await allow(await popup()); expect(requests).toBe(2); await page.unroute('**/api/mcp/connections/*/authorize')
  })
  test('public and confidential pre-registered clients are write-only and connect', async () => {
    for (const [name, slug, id] of [['Public docs', 'public-docs', 'public'], ['Private docs', 'private-docs', 'confidential']]) {
      await add(name, slug, 'oauth_client'); const editor = card(name).getByRole('region', { name: 'Pre-registered OAuth client', exact: true })
      await expect(editor.getByText(`${collector.origin}/api/mcp/oauth/callback`, { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
      await editor.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Client ID', exact: true }).fill(id)
      if (id === 'confidential') {
        await editor.getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Client secret action', exact: true }).selectOption('replace'); await editor.getByLabel('Client secret', { exact: true }).fill(SECRET)
        let refused = false
        await page.route('**/api/mcp/connections/*/oauth-client', async route => {
          if (!refused) { refused = true; await route.fulfill({ status: 403, contentType: 'application/json', body: JSON.stringify({ code: 'step_up_required' }) }) }
          else await route.continue()
        })
      }
      await editor.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save client', exact: true }).click()
      if (id === 'confidential') {
        const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'This needs a fresh confirmation', exact: true }); await expect(dialog).toBeVisible()
        await expect(editor.getByLabel('Client secret', { exact: true })).toHaveValue('')
        expect(await page.locator('input, textarea').evaluateAll((fields, secret) => fields.some(field => (field as HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement).value.includes(secret)), SECRET)).toBe(false)
        await dialog.getByLabel('Your password', { exact: true }).fill(PASSWORD); await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm', exact: true }).click()
        await page.unroute('**/api/mcp/connections/*/oauth-client')
      }
      await expect(editor.getByRole('status')).toHaveText('Client saved. Connect to authorize it.')
      expect(await page.locator('input[type="password"]').evaluateAll((fields, secret) => fields.some(field => (field as HTMLInputElement).value.includes(secret)), SECRET)).toBe(false)
      await allow(await popup(name, 'Connect'), name); const c = await listed(slug)
      expect(c.oauth.has_client_secret).toBe(id === 'confidential'); expect(c.has_credential).toBe(true)
    }
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => JSON.stringify({ ...localStorage, ...sessionStorage }))).not.toContain(SECRET)
  })
  test('stale resource acceptance refetches a new comparison before connecting', async () => {
    vendor.config.resourcePath = '/different'; await add('Resource docs', 'resource-docs')
    await card('Resource docs').getByRole('button', { name: 'Connect', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(card('Resource docs').getByText(`${vendor.origin}/different`, { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    vendor.config.resourcePath = '/changed'; await card('Resource docs').getByRole('button', { name: 'Accept and connect', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(card('Resource docs').getByText(`${vendor.origin}/changed`, { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await allow(await popup('Resource docs', 'Accept and connect'), 'Resource docs')
    expect((await listed('resource-docs')).oauth.accepted_resource).toBe(`${vendor.origin}/changed`)
    vendor.config.resourcePath = '/mcp'
  })
  test('probe refreshes real access, then revocation yields needs_auth with an attention badge', async () => {
    vendor.expire(); await card().getByRole('button', { name: 'Probe now', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(card().getByText('Probe answered. The card shows the gateway’s report.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    expect(vendor.counts.refreshes).toBe(1); expect((await listed()).status).toBe('ok')
    vendor.revoke(); await page.waitForTimeout(10500)
    await card().getByRole('button', { name: 'Probe now', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(card().getByText('May need sign-in', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    const attention = page.locator(width === 390 ? '.mtopbar' : '.rail-nav').getByText('1 need attention', { exact: true })
    await expect(attention).toBeVisible(); await expect(attention).toHaveCSS('color', 'rgb(122, 83, 0)')
    if (width === 390) expect((await page.locator('.mtopbar .mcp-attention-link').boundingBox())!.height).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(44)
  })
  test('issuer changes require a deliberate client save and keep the old grant until consent', async () => {
    vendor.config.issuerPath = '/tenant'
    await card('Public docs').getByRole('button', { name: 'Reconnect', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(card('Public docs').getByText('The authorization server changed. Review and save the vendor client again before connecting.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    const editor = card('Public docs').getByRole('region', { name: 'Pre-registered OAuth client', exact: true })
    await editor.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save client', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(editor.getByText('Saved settings take effect after a successful Connect. The current grant stays in use meanwhile. The pending client uses the vendor token endpoint it is pinned to at the next Connect.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    expect((await listed('public-docs')).has_credential).toBe(true)
    await allow(await popup('Public docs'), 'Public docs')
    expect((await listed('public-docs')).oauth.pending_client).toBeUndefined()
    vendor.config.issuerPath = ''
  })
  test('foreign resources are refused without an acceptance action', async () => {
    vendor.config.resourcePath = 'https://other.example/mcp'; await add('Foreign docs', 'foreign-docs')
    await card('Foreign docs').getByRole('button', { name: 'Connect', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(card('Foreign docs').getByText('The advertised resource cannot be accepted. Check the server URL.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await expect(card('Foreign docs').getByRole('button', { name: 'Accept and connect', exact: true })).toHaveCount(0)
    expect((await listed('foreign-docs')).has_credential).toBe(false); vendor.config.resourcePath = '/mcp'
  })
  test('a vendor without registration suggests pre-registered settings', async () => {
    vendor.config.registration = false; await add('Registered docs', 'registered-docs')
    await card('Registered docs').getByRole('button', { name: 'Connect', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(card('Registered docs').getByText('This vendor does not offer automatic registration. Edit the connection to use a pre-registered client.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    expect((await listed('registered-docs')).has_credential).toBe(false); vendor.config.registration = true
  })
  test('probe outage shows error without reconnect, preserves its cooldown report, and recovers', async () => {
    await add('Outage docs', 'outage-docs'); await allow(await popup('Outage docs', 'Connect'), 'Outage docs')
    vendor.config.mcpStatus = 503; await card('Outage docs').getByRole('button', { name: 'Probe now', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(card('Outage docs').getByText('Is failing', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await expect(card('Outage docs').getByRole('button', { name: 'Reconnect', exact: true })).toHaveCount(0)
    const failure = await listed('outage-docs'); expect(failure.status_note).toBeTruthy()
    await expect(card('Outage docs').getByText(failure.status_note, { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    vendor.config.mcpStatus = null
    await card('Outage docs').getByRole('button', { name: 'Probe now', exact: true }).click()
    expect((await listed('outage-docs')).status).toBe('error')
    await page.waitForTimeout(10500)
    await card('Outage docs').getByRole('button', { name: 'Probe now', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(card('Outage docs').getByText('Working', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    const working = await listed('outage-docs')
    vendor.config.mcpStatus = 400; await page.waitForTimeout(10500)
    await card('Outage docs').getByRole('button', { name: 'Probe now', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(async () => { const unchanged = await listed('outage-docs'); expect(unchanged.status).toBe('ok'); expect(Date.parse(unchanged.checked_at)).toBeGreaterThan(Date.parse(working.checked_at)) }).toPass()
    vendor.config.mcpStatus = null
  })
  test('callback refusal is served without an operator cookie under its own CSP', async ({ browser }) => {
    const context = await browser.newContext()
    try {
      const callback = await context.newPage()
      const response = await callback.goto(`${collector.origin}/api/mcp/oauth/callback?state=unknown&code=never-echo-this-code`)
      expect(response!.status()).toBe(400)
      expect(response!.headers()['content-security-policy']).toContain("script-src 'self'")
      expect(response!.headers()['cache-control']).toBe('no-store')
      await callback.waitForURL(`${collector.origin}/api/mcp/oauth/callback`)
      await expect(callback.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Not connected', exact: true })).toBeVisible()
      await expect(callback.getByRole('button', { name: 'Close', exact: true })).toBeVisible()
      expect(await callback.content()).not.toContain('never-echo-this-code')
      expect(await callback.locator('body').getAttribute('data-connection')).toBe('')
    } finally { await context.close() }
  })
})
````

Write `web/e2e/mcp.spec.ts`:

````typescript
import { captureManagement } from './management-captures'
// The MCP gateway's connections in a real browser, against the real binary
// (plan 4e-i): a connection created with a step-up, its token set with a
// step-up and gone from the page at once, and mounted on a host paired with
// the command the Hosts screen shows, at 1280 px and at 390 px, each width
// with a collector and a host of its own.
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test'
import { mkdtempSync, readdirSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs'
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { startCollector, type Collector } from './collector'
import { scratchEnv } from './spawn'
import { testHost, type TestHost } from './host'

const PASSWORD = 'correct horse battery staple'
const TOKEN = 'e2e-token-0123456789abcdef'

/** Answers the first `method` request to a URL matching `pattern` with 403
 *  `step_up_required`, as the server would five minutes after a sign-in,
 *  and lets every other request through. Resolves the count of refusals. */
async function refuseOnce(page: Page, pattern: string, method: string): Promise<() => number> {
  let refused = 0
  await page.route(pattern, async (route) => {
    if (route.request().method() === method && refused === 0) {
      refused++
      await route.fulfill({
        status: 403,
        contentType: 'application/json',
        body: JSON.stringify({ code: 'step_up_required', message: 'confirm it is you' }),
      })
    } else {
      await route.continue()
    }
  })
  return () => refused
}

async function stepUp(page: Page) {
  const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'This needs a fresh confirmation', exact: true })
  await expect(dialog).toBeVisible()
  await dialog.getByLabel('Your password', { exact: true }).fill(PASSWORD)
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm', exact: true }).click()
  await expect(dialog).toBeHidden()
}

for (const width of [1280, 390]) {
  test.describe(`at ${width} px`, () => {
    test.describe.configure({ mode: 'serial' })

    let collector: Collector
    let host: TestHost
    let page: Page
    const violations: string[] = []
    let sentinel: string
    let runnerLogDir: string | undefined

    test.beforeAll(async ({ browser }) => {
      sentinel = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'hennery-e2e-sentinel-'))
      runnerLogDir = process.env.HENNERY_LOG_DIR
      process.env.HENNERY_LOG_DIR = sentinel
      expect(scratchEnv(sentinel).HENNERY_LOG_DIR).toBeUndefined()
      collector = await startCollector()
      host = testHost()
      const context = await browser.newContext({ baseURL: collector.origin, viewport: { width, height: 844 } })
      page = await context.newPage()
      await page.addInitScript(() => {
        document.addEventListener('securitypolicyviolation', (e) => {
          const seen = ((window as unknown as { __csp?: string[] }).__csp ??= [])
          seen.push(`${e.violatedDirective} ${e.blockedURI}`)
        })
      })
      page.on('console', (m) => {
        if (m.text().includes('Content Security Policy')) violations.push(m.text())
      })
      const token = new URL(collector.setupLink).hash.slice(1)
      const setup = await page.request.post('/api/setup', {
        headers: { Origin: collector.origin },
        data: { token, password: PASSWORD, public_url: collector.origin },
      })
      expect(setup.status()).toBe(201)
    })

    test.afterAll(async () => {
      try {
        await page?.context().close()
      } finally {
        try {
          await host?.stop()
        } finally {
          try {
            await collector?.stop()
          } finally {
            if (runnerLogDir === undefined) delete process.env.HENNERY_LOG_DIR
            else process.env.HENNERY_LOG_DIR = runnerLogDir
            if (sentinel) rmSync(sentinel, { recursive: true, force: true })
          }
        }
      }
    })

    test('a host is paired to mount connections on', async () => {
      await page.goto('/hosts')
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add host', exact: true }).click()
      const command = (await page.getByLabel('Pairing command', { exact: true }).textContent())!
      expect(await host.join(command, ['--name', 'e2e host', '--no-runtime'])).toBe(0)
      await expect(page.getByText('Paired: e2e host', { exact: true })).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15_000 })
    })

    test('a connection is created after a fresh confirmation', async () => {
      await page.goto('/mcp')
      await expect(page.getByRole('region', { name: 'How sessions get these', exact: true })).toContainText(
        'Changes apply to new and resumed sessions.',
      )
      const refused = await refuseOnce(page, '**/api/mcp/connections', 'POST')
      await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add connection', exact: true }).click()
      const form = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Add a connection', exact: true })
      await form.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Name', exact: true }).fill('Docs')
      await form.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Slug', exact: true }).fill('docs')
      await form.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Server URL', exact: true }).fill('https://mcp.example.com/mcp')
      await captureManagement(page, 'mcp-add', 'Desktop mcp / add connection')
      await form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create', exact: true }).click()
      await stepUp(page)
      const card = page.getByRole('listitem', { name: 'Docs', exact: true })
      await expect(card.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Docs', exact: true })).toBeFocused()
      await expect(card.getByText('hennery-docs', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
      await expect(card.getByText('Not used yet', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
      await expect(card.getByText('Not set yet', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
      expect(refused()).toBe(1)
      await page.unroute('**/api/mcp/connections')
    })

    test('its token is set after a fresh confirmation, and is gone from the page at once', async () => {
      const card = page.getByRole('listitem', { name: 'Docs', exact: true })
      const refused = await refuseOnce(page, '**/api/mcp/connections/*/credential', 'PUT')
      const field = card.getByLabel('Token', { exact: true })
      await field.fill(TOKEN)
      await card.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save token', exact: true }).click()
      await expect(field).toHaveValue('')
      await stepUp(page)
      await expect(card.getByRole('status')).toHaveText('Token saved. It is never shown again.')
      await expect(card.getByText('Set', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
      // Focus is back where the operator left it, not on the page's body.
      await expect(card.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save token', exact: true })).toBeFocused()
      expect(refused()).toBe(1)
      await page.unroute('**/api/mcp/connections/*/credential')
      expect(await page.content()).not.toContain(TOKEN)
      expect(page.url()).not.toContain(TOKEN)
      expect(await page.evaluate(() => JSON.stringify({ ...localStorage, ...sessionStorage }))).not.toContain(TOKEN)
      // The server answers whether one is stored, never the token.
      const listed = await page.request.get('/api/mcp/connections', { headers: { Origin: collector.origin } })
      expect(listed.status()).toBe(200)
      const body = await listed.text()
      expect(body).not.toContain(TOKEN)
      expect(JSON.parse(body)).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ slug: 'docs', has_credential: true })])
    })

    test('it is mounted on the host, and stays so after a reload', async () => {
      const card = page.getByRole('listitem', { name: 'Docs', exact: true })
      const tick = card.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'e2e host', exact: true })
      await expect(tick).not.toBeChecked()
      // Ticked once the server's answer lands, not before: a click, not
      // check(), which wants the box ticked at once.
      await captureManagement(page, 'mcp-token-mounts', 'Desktop mcp / gateway mounts')
      await tick.click()
      await expect(tick).toBeChecked()
      await expect(tick).toBeFocused()
      await expect(page.getByRole('dialog')).toHaveCount(0)
      await page.reload()
      await expect(page.getByRole('listitem', { name: 'Docs', exact: true }).getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'e2e host', exact: true })).toBeChecked()
    })

    test('broke no Content-Security-Policy rule', async () => {
      expect(violations).toEqual([])
      const seen = await page.evaluate(() => (window as unknown as { __csp?: string[] }).__csp ?? [])
      expect(seen).toEqual([])
    })

    test('wrote nothing where the runner’s own environment pointed', async () => {
      expect(readdirSync(sentinel)).toEqual([])
    })
  })
}
````

Write `web/src/components/AccountSettings.tsx`:

````tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { passkeys, registerPasskey, removePasskey } from '../api/auth'
import { authDevices, revokeDevice } from '../api/device'
import { messageOf } from '../api/errors'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import { useResource } from '../hooks/useResource'
import { Text } from '../lib/text'
import { completeLogout } from '../pwa/logout'
import ConfirmDialog from './ConfirmDialog'
import SignOut from './SignOut'
import When from './When'
import type { PasskeyItem } from '../generated/protocol'

export default function AccountSettings() {
  const client = useClient()
  const keys = useResource(() => passkeys(client), [client])
  const logins = useResource(() => authDevices(client), [client])
  const [label, setLabel] = useState(''), [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null), [remove, setRemove] = useState<PasskeyItem | null>(null)
  const supported = typeof PublicKeyCredential !== 'undefined' && !!navigator.credentials?.create
  const add = async () => {
    setBusy(true); setError(null)
    try { const key = await registerPasskey(client, label.trim()); keys.set(rows => [...(rows ?? []), key]); setLabel('') }
    catch (err) { setError(messageOf(err)) } finally { setBusy(false) }
  }
  return <>
    <section className="card" aria-labelledby="sign-in-heading">
      <h2 id="sign-in-heading" tabIndex={-1}>Sign-in and passkeys</h2>
      <p>Sensitive changes ask you to confirm with your password or a passkey.</p>
      <p className="hint">To reset a password, use <code>hennery admin reset-password</code> on the collector. This removes passkeys and ends every login. Changing the public host name also invalidates passkeys.</p>
      {keys.error && <p role="alert">Unable to load passkeys. <Text>{keys.error}</Text> <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={keys.reload}>Retry passkeys</button></p>}
      {!keys.data && !keys.error && <p role="status">Loading passkeys…</p>}
      {keys.data?.length === 0 && <p>No passkeys registered.</p>}
      <ul className="cards" aria-label="Passkeys">{keys.data?.map(key => <li className="card" key={key.id}>
        <h3><Text>{key.label}</Text></h3>
        <dl className="facts"><dt>Created</dt><dd><When at={key.created_at} /></dd><dt>Last used</dt><dd>{key.last_used_at ? <When at={key.last_used_at} /> : 'Not used yet'}</dd></dl>
        <button className="btn btn-danger" onClick={() => setRemove(key)}>Remove <Text>{key.label}</Text></button>
      </li>)}</ul>
      <label className="field">Passkey label<input className="input" maxLength={64} value={label} onChange={e => setLabel(e.target.value)} disabled={busy} /></label>
      <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy || !supported || !keys.data} onClick={() => void add()}>Add passkey</button>
      {!supported && <p className="hint">Passkeys are unavailable in this browser. Use your password to sign in.</p>}
      {error && <p role="alert"><Text>{error}</Text></p>}
    </section>
    <section className="card" aria-labelledby="logins-heading"><h2 id="logins-heading">Signed-in devices</h2><p className="hint">Each row is a login. Revoke also removes its push registrations.</p>
      {logins.error && <p role="alert">Unable to load signed-in devices. <Text>{logins.error}</Text> <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={logins.reload}>Retry devices</button></p>}
      {!logins.data && !logins.error && <p role="status">Loading signed-in devices…</p>}
      <ul className="cards">{logins.data?.map(row => <li key={row.id} className="card"><p><Text>{row.user_agent}</Text>{row.current && ' (current login)'}</p>
        <dl className="facts"><dt>Created</dt><dd><When at={row.created_at} /></dd><dt>Last seen</dt><dd><When at={row.last_seen_at} /></dd><dt>Expires</dt><dd><When at={row.expires_at} /></dd></dl>
        <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => { setBusy(true); setError(null); void revokeDevice(client, row.id).then(async () => { if (row.current) await completeLogout(client); else logins.reload() }).catch(err => setError(messageOf(err))).finally(() => setBusy(false)) }}>Revoke {row.current ? 'current login' : 'login'}</button>
      </li>)}</ul>
    </section>
    <section className="card"><h2>This login</h2><SignOut className="btn btn-ghost" /></section>
    {remove && <ConfirmDialog title="Remove this passkey?" confirm="Remove passkey" danger returnFocus={() => document.getElementById('sign-in-heading')} onClose={() => setRemove(null)} action={async () => { await removePasskey(client, remove.id); keys.set(rows => (rows ?? []).filter(key => key.id !== remove.id)) }}>
      <p>Remove <Text>{remove.label}</Text>? You can still sign in with your password or another passkey.</p>
    </ConfirmDialog>}
  </>
}
````

Write `web/src/components/CollectorSettings.tsx`:

````tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { saveContact } from '../api/device'
import { settings } from '../api/manage'
import { messageOf } from '../api/errors'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import type { AttachmentUsage, HatItem, HostRemovalItem, RemovalState } from '../generated/protocol'
import { useResource } from '../hooks/useResource'
import { Text } from '../lib/text'
import { Link } from '../router'

const REMOVAL: Record<RemovalState, string> = {
  removed: 'Transcript removed',
  partial: 'Transcript removed in part',
  none: 'No agent transcript record',
  pending: 'Transcript removal unconfirmed',
}

export default function CollectorSettings({ push, sessions, hats }: { push: boolean; sessions: boolean; hats: HatItem[] | null }) {
  const client = useClient()
  const config = useResource(() => settings(client), [client])
  const usage = useResource(() => sessions ? client.request<AttachmentUsage>('GET', '/api/settings/attachments') : Promise.resolve(null), [client, sessions])
  const removals = useResource(() => sessions ? client.request<HostRemovalItem[]>('GET', '/api/settings/host-removals') : Promise.resolve(null), [client, sessions])
  const [contact, setContact] = useState<string | null>(null), [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null), [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null)
  return <section className="card" aria-labelledby="collector-heading"><h2 id="collector-heading">Collector</h2>
    {config.error && <p role="alert">Unable to load collector information. <Text>{config.error}</Text> <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={config.reload}>Retry collector</button></p>}
    {!config.data && !config.error && <p role="status">Loading collector information…</p>}
    <dl className="facts"><dt>Public URL</dt><dd>{config.data ? <Text>{config.data.public_url}</Text> : 'Not loaded'}</dd>
      {sessions && <><dt>Default for new hosts</dt><dd>{hats ? <Text>{hats.find(hat => hat.default_for_new_hosts)?.name ?? 'None reported'}</Text> : 'Not loaded'}</dd></>}
      {usage.data && <><dt>Attachments</dt><dd>{usage.data.count} images · {usage.data.bytes.toLocaleString()} bytes</dd></>}
    </dl>
    <p className="hint">The public URL is managed on the collector. <code>hennery admin reset-public-url</code> ends logins and push registrations; a changed host name removes passkeys.</p>
    {sessions && <p><Link to="/hats">Manage hats</Link> · <Link to="/hosts">Per-host defaults</Link></p>}
    {push && config.data && <div className="collector-contact"><p className="hint">The push contact lets a notification service contact the collector operator.</p>
      <label className="field">Push contact<input className="input" disabled={busy} value={contact ?? config.data.contact ?? ''} onChange={e => setContact(e.target.value)} /></label>
      <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => { setBusy(true); setError(null); setNotice(null); void saveContact(client, contact ?? config.data!.contact ?? '').then(value => { setContact(value.contact ?? ''); setNotice('Contact saved.') }).catch(err => setError(messageOf(err))).finally(() => setBusy(false)) }}>Save contact</button>
      {error && <p role="alert"><Text>{error}</Text></p>}{notice && <p role="status">{notice}</p>}
    </div>}
    {sessions && <><h3>Host transcript cleanup</h3>
      {usage.error && <p role="alert">Attachment usage could not be loaded. <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={usage.reload}>Retry usage</button></p>}
      {removals.error && <p role="alert">Cleanup status could not be loaded. <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={removals.reload}>Retry cleanup</button></p>}
      {removals.data?.length === 0 && <p>No host cleanup records reported.</p>}
      {!removals.data && !removals.error && <p role="status">Loading cleanup status…</p>}
      <ul className="cards">{removals.data?.map(row => <li className="card" key={row.id}><p><Text>{row.agent}</Text>: {row.state === 'pending' ? 'Waiting for the host to retry' : row.last_result ? REMOVAL[row.last_result.state] : 'No cleanup result reported'}</p>
        <p>{row.attempts} attempts · {row.last_result ? `${row.last_result.remaining.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.count, 0)} entries reported remaining` : 'No cleanup result reported yet'}</p>
      </li>)}</ul>
      <p className="hint">Agents run as the host’s OS user. Use a separate OS user for stronger isolation. Cleanup is best effort; copies outside the reported transcript may remain.</p>
    </>}
  </section>
}
````

Write `web/src/components/DelegationSettings.test.tsx`:

````tsx
import { act, render, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react'
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event'
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { Client } from '../api/client'
import { ClientContext } from '../app-client'
import { StepUpCancelled } from '../api/errors'
import { json, stubServer, type Answer } from '../test-server'
import type { HostCatalog } from '../generated/protocol'
import DelegationSettings from './DelegationSettings'

const HAT = { id: 'hat', name: 'Personal', colour: '#64748b', default_for_new_hosts: true, created_at: '2026-10-20T09:00:00Z', purging: false }
const catalog: HostCatalog = { agent: 'claude', adapter_revision: 'pin1', freshness: 'fresh', security: { known: true, axes: { mode: ['default', 'plan'] } }, defaults: { mode: 'default' }, config_options: [
  { id: 'permission', category: 'mode', name: 'Permissions', type: 'select', currentValue: 'default', options: [{ value: 'default', name: 'Default' }, { value: 'plan', name: 'Plan' }, { value: 'bypass', name: 'Bypass permissions' }] },
  { id: 'effort', name: 'Effort', type: 'select', currentValue: 'low', options: [{ value: 'low', name: 'Low' }] },
] }
const BASE = { revision: 1, allowed: { other: { sandbox: ['strict'], audit: [false] } } }
function open(routes: Record<string, Answer | Answer[]> = {}, stepUp = vi.fn(async () => {})) {
  const server = stubServer({
    'GET /api/hats/hat/session-tools': json(200, { delegation_enabled: false, revision: 1 }),
    'GET /api/hats/hat/delegation-policy': json(200, BASE),
    'GET /api/hosts': json(200, [{ host_id: 'host', name: 'Workstation', connected: true }]),
    'GET /api/hosts/host/runtime-catalog?agent=claude&cwd=%2Fproject': json(200, catalog),
    'PATCH /api/hats/hat/session-tools': json(200, { delegation_enabled: true, revision: 2 }),
    'PATCH /api/hats/hat/delegation-policy': json(200, { revision: 2, allowed: { ...BASE.allowed, claude: { mode: ['default', 'plan', 'bypass'] } } }),
    ...routes,
  })
  const client = new Client({ fetch: server.fetch, here: () => ({ pathname: '/settings', search: '' }), navigate: vi.fn(), stepUp })
  render(<ClientContext.Provider value={client}><DelegationSettings hats={[HAT]} /></ClientContext.Provider>)
  return { server, stepUp }
}
async function context() {
  await screen.findByRole('option', { name: 'Workstation' })
  await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Grant host', { exact: true }), 'host')
  await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Grant agent', { exact: true })).toBeEnabled())
  await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Grant agent', { exact: true }), 'claude')
  await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Grant project path', { exact: true }), '/project')
  await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Load grant choices' }))
  return screen.findByRole('group', { name: 'Permissions' })
}
async function edit() {
  const group = await context()
  await userEvent.click(within(group).getByLabelText('Override Permissions', { exact: true }))
  await userEvent.click(within(group).getByLabelText('Bypass permissions', { exact: true }))
  return group
}
describe('delegation Settings', () => {
  it('locks a pending toggle and displays the returned flag rather than an optimistic guess', async () => {
    let finish!: (response: Response) => void
    open({ 'PATCH /api/hats/hat/session-tools': () => new Promise<Response>(resolve => { finish = resolve }) })
    const toggle = await screen.findByLabelText('Enable delegation session tools', { exact: true })
    await userEvent.click(toggle)
    expect(toggle).toBeDisabled()
    expect(toggle).not.toBeChecked()
    await act(async () => finish(json(200, { delegation_enabled: false, revision: 7 })))
    expect(toggle).not.toBeChecked()
    expect(toggle).toBeEnabled()
  })
  it('changes only session tools, waits for saved state and explains activation and disable behavior', async () => {
    const { server } = open()
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByLabelText('Enable delegation session tools', { exact: true }))
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Enable delegation session tools', { exact: true })).toBeChecked())
    expect(server.sent.filter(r => r.method === 'PATCH').map(r => [r.path, r.body])).toEqual([['/api/hats/hat/session-tools', { delegation_enabled: true }]])
    expect(screen.getByText('Disabling immediately denies built-in calls, cancels work not yet dispatched and routes outstanding attention to you. Already delivered work keeps running. Re-enabling does not replay cancelled work.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
  })
  it('restores the saved toggle on step-up cancel without a retry', async () => {
    const stepUp = vi.fn(async () => { throw new StepUpCancelled() })
    const { server } = open({ 'PATCH /api/hats/hat/session-tools': json(403, { code: 'step_up_required' }) }, stepUp)
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByLabelText('Enable delegation session tools', { exact: true }))
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Change cancelled')
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Enable delegation session tools', { exact: true })).not.toBeChecked()
    expect(stepUp).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
    expect(server.sent.filter(r => r.method === 'PATCH')).toHaveLength(1)
  })
  it('uses trusted security ids, preserves unrelated grants and saves only after review with step-up', async () => {
    const { server, stepUp } = open({ 'PATCH /api/hats/hat/delegation-policy': [json(403, { code: 'step_up_required' }), json(200, { revision: 9, allowed: { ...BASE.allowed, claude: { mode: ['default', 'plan', 'bypass'] } } })] })
    await edit()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('group', { name: 'Effort' })).toBeNull()
    expect(server.sent.some(r => r.method === 'PATCH')).toBe(false)
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Review execution grants' }))
    const review = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Save execution grants?' })
    expect(review).toHaveTextContent('other · sandbox: strict')
    expect(review).toHaveTextContent('Claude · Permissions: Default, Plan, Bypass permissions')
    await userEvent.click(within(review).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save execution grants' }))
    await waitFor(() => expect(review).not.toBeInTheDocument())
    expect(stepUp).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
    const writes = server.sent.filter(r => r.method === 'PATCH')
    expect(writes.map(r => r.body)).toEqual(Array(2).fill({ allowed: { ...BASE.allowed, claude: { mode: ['default', 'plan', 'bypass'] } } }))
    expect(await screen.findByText(/^Saved version 9\..*$/)).toBeVisible()
  })
  it('keeps policy input on cancellation/refusal and never expands tools or grants automatically', async () => {
    const { server } = open({ 'PATCH /api/hats/hat/delegation-policy': json(403, { code: 'step_up_required' }) }, vi.fn(async () => { throw new StepUpCancelled() }))
    await edit()
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Review execution grants' }))
    const review = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Save execution grants?' })
    await userEvent.click(within(review).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save execution grants' }))
    expect(await within(review).findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Not confirmed.')
    await userEvent.click(within(review).getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }))
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Bypass permissions', { exact: true })).toBeChecked()
    expect(server.sent.filter(r => r.method === 'PATCH')).toHaveLength(1)
  })
  it('requires explicit axis selection for unknown profiles', async () => {
    open({ 'GET /api/hosts/host/runtime-catalog?agent=claude&cwd=%2Fproject': json(200, { ...catalog, security: { known: false, axes: {} } }) })
    const group = await context()
    expect(within(group).getByLabelText('Bypass permissions', { exact: true })).toBeDisabled()
    await userEvent.click(within(group).getByLabelText('Use as policy setting Permissions', { exact: true }))
    expect(within(group).getByLabelText('Default', { exact: true })).not.toBeChecked()
    expect(within(group).getByLabelText('Bypass permissions', { exact: true })).toBeEnabled()
    expect(screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Effort' })).toBeVisible()
  })
  it('stale catalogs cannot add choices but existing unavailable grants can be removed deliberately', async () => {
    open({ 'GET /api/hosts/host/runtime-catalog?agent=claude&cwd=%2Fproject': json(200, { ...catalog, freshness: 'stale' }),
      'GET /api/hats/hat/delegation-policy': json(200, { revision: 1, allowed: { claude: { mode: ['retired'] }, other: BASE.allowed.other } }),
    })
    const group = await context()
    expect(within(group).getByLabelText('Override Permissions', { exact: true })).toBeDisabled()
    expect(within(group).getByLabelText('Bypass permissions', { exact: true })).toBeDisabled()
    expect(screen.getByText('Some saved values are unavailable in this context. They stay granted until you remove them below.', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove override: Claude / Permissions' }))
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Review execution grants' }))
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog')).toHaveTextContent('other · sandbox: strict')
  })
  it('fences a catalog that returns after the context changes', async () => {
    let finish!: (r: Response) => void
    open({ 'GET /api/hosts/host/runtime-catalog?agent=claude&cwd=%2Fproject': () => new Promise<Response>(resolve => { finish = resolve }) })
    await screen.findByRole('option', { name: 'Workstation' })
  await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Grant host', { exact: true }), 'host')
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Grant agent', { exact: true })).toBeEnabled())
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Grant agent', { exact: true }), 'claude')
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Grant project path', { exact: true }), '/project')
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Load grant choices' }))
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Grant project path', { exact: true }), '/changed')
    await act(async () => finish(json(200, catalog)))
    expect(screen.queryByRole('group', { name: 'Permissions' })).toBeNull()
  })
  it('independently displays tools and policy failures with retry, and never labels failure as disabled', async () => {
    open({ 'GET /api/hats/hat/session-tools': json(500, { message: 'Temporarily unavailable' }) })
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Unable to load session tools')
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Enable delegation session tools', { exact: true })).toBeNull()
    expect(await screen.findByText('strict', { exact: true })).toBeVisible()
  })
})
````

Write `web/src/components/DelegationSettings.tsx`:

````tsx
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { executionPolicy, runtimeCatalog, sessionTools, setExecutionPolicy, setSessionTools } from '../api/delegation'
import { hosts as listHosts } from '../api/manage'
import { messageOf, StepUpCancelled } from '../api/errors'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import type { ExecutionPolicy, HatItem, HostCatalog } from '../generated/protocol'
import { useResource } from '../hooks/useResource'
import { policyChoices, policyFields, replaceGrant } from '../lib/delegationPolicy'
import { agentLabel } from '../lib/agent'
import { Text, visible } from '../lib/text'
import ConfirmDialog from './ConfirmDialog'

export default function DelegationSettings({ hats }: { hats: HatItem[] | null }) {
  const id = useId()
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState('')
  const hat = hats?.find(h => h.id === selected) ?? hats?.find(h => !h.purging)
  return <section className="card" aria-labelledby="delegation-heading">
    <h2 id="delegation-heading" tabIndex={-1}>Delegation by hat</h2>
    <p>Let an agent start and coordinate child sessions. Session tools and execution grants are separate choices.</p>
    {!hats && <p role="status">Loading hats…</p>}
    {hats?.length === 0 && <p>No hats available.</p>}
    {hat && <><div className="field"><label htmlFor={id}>Delegation hat</label><select id={id} value={hat.id} onChange={e => setSelected(e.target.value)}>{hats?.map(h => <option key={h.id} value={h.id}>{visible(h.name)}</option>)}</select></div>
      <HatDelegation key={hat.id} hat={hat} /></>}
  </section>
}

function HatDelegation({ hat }: { hat: HatItem }) {
  const client = useClient()
  const tools = useResource(() => sessionTools(client, hat.id), [client, hat.id])
  const policy = useResource(() => executionPolicy(client, hat.id), [client, hat.id])
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false), [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null)
  return <div>
    <h3><Text>{hat.name}</Text></h3>
    {hat.purging && <p role="alert">This hat is being purged. Delegation settings are read-only.</p>}
    {tools.error && <p role="alert">Unable to load session tools. <Text>{tools.error}</Text> <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={tools.reload}>Retry session tools</button></p>}
    {!tools.data && !tools.error && <p role="status">Loading session tools…</p>}
    {tools.data && <><label className="policy-control"><input type="checkbox" checked={tools.data.delegation_enabled} disabled={busy || hat.purging} onChange={e => {
      const enabled = e.target.checked
      setBusy(true); setError(null); setNotice(null)
      void setSessionTools(client, hat.id, { delegation_enabled: enabled }).then(saved => {
        tools.set(saved); setNotice(saved.delegation_enabled ? 'Delegation enabled. Start or resume a session to receive the tools.' : 'Delegation disabled. Already delivered work continues as ordinary sessions.')
      }).catch(err => setError(err instanceof StepUpCancelled ? 'Change cancelled. The saved setting is unchanged.' : messageOf(err))).finally(() => setBusy(false))
    }} />Enable delegation session tools</label><p className="hint">Saved version {tools.data.revision}. Enabling takes effect at the next start or resume. It does not expand execution grants.</p></>}
    <p className="hint">Disabling immediately denies built-in calls, cancels work not yet dispatched and routes outstanding attention to you. Already delivered work keeps running. Re-enabling does not replay cancelled work.</p>
    {error && <p role="alert"><Text>{error}</Text></p>}{notice && <p role="status">{notice}</p>}
    <h3>Execution grants</h3>
    <p>Known profiles allow their baseline settings without an override: Claude Default or Plan, Codex Read only. More permissive settings need an explicit grant. Other profiles need explicit policy settings. Availability of the tools also depends on the host and agent.</p>
    {policy.error && <p role="alert">Unable to load execution grants. <Text>{policy.error}</Text> <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={policy.reload}>Retry grants</button></p>}
    {!policy.data && !policy.error && <p role="status">Loading execution grants…</p>}
    {policy.data && <GrantEditor key={`${hat.id}:${policy.data.revision}`} hat={hat} saved={policy.data} onSaved={policy.set} />}
  </div>
}

function GrantEditor({ hat, saved, onSaved }: { hat: HatItem; saved: ExecutionPolicy; onSaved: (value: ExecutionPolicy) => void }) {
  const client = useClient()
  const id = useId()
  const hosts = useResource(() => listHosts(client), [client])
  const [host, setHost] = useState(''), [agent, setAgent] = useState(''), [cwd, setCwd] = useState('')
  const [catalog, setCatalog] = useState<HostCatalog | null>(null), [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null), [review, setReview] = useState(false)
  const [allowed, setAllowed] = useState(saved.allowed)
  const controller = useRef<AbortController | null>(null)
  useEffect(() => () => controller.current?.abort(), [])
  const resetContext = () => { controller.current?.abort(); setCatalog(null); setLoading(false); setError(null) }
  const online = hosts.data?.some(h => h.host_id === host && h.connected && !h.revoked_at) ?? false
  const load = async () => {
    controller.current?.abort()
    const current = new AbortController(); controller.current = current
    setCatalog(null); setLoading(true); setError(null)
    try {
      const next = await runtimeCatalog(client, host, agent, cwd.trim(), current.signal)
      if (!current.signal.aborted) {
        if (next.agent !== agent) throw new Error('The agent changed. Load its choices again.')
        setCatalog(next)
      }
    } catch (err) { if (!current.signal.aborted) setError(messageOf(err)) }
    finally { if (!current.signal.aborted) setLoading(false) }
  }
  const fields = catalog ? policyFields(catalog) : []
  const usable = catalog?.freshness === 'fresh' && online && !loading && !hat.purging
  const changed = JSON.stringify(allowed) !== JSON.stringify(saved.allowed)
  const runtimeGrants = Object.hasOwn(allowed, agent) ? allowed[agent] : {}
  return <div className="execution-grants">
    <p className="hint">Saved version {saved.revision}. An override replaces all allowed values for that setting. Removing it returns to the profile baseline. Saving never starts or retries a child session.</p>
    <div className="settings-context">
      <div className="field"><label htmlFor={`${id}-host`}>Grant host</label><select id={`${id}-host`} value={host} onChange={e => { resetContext(); setHost(e.target.value); setAgent('') }}><option value="">Choose a host</option>{hosts.data?.filter(h => !h.revoked_at).map(h => <option key={h.host_id} value={h.host_id} disabled={!h.connected}>{visible(h.name)}{!h.connected && ' (offline)'}</option>)}</select></div>
      <div className="field"><label htmlFor={`${id}-agent`}>Grant agent</label><input className="input" id={`${id}-agent`} list={`${id}-agents`} value={agent} maxLength={128} disabled={!host} onChange={e => { resetContext(); setAgent(e.target.value) }} placeholder="Agent id configured on this host" aria-describedby={`${id}-agent-hint`} />
        <datalist id={`${id}-agents`}>{Object.keys(hosts.data?.find(h => h.host_id === host)?.mcp_delivery ?? {}).map(runtime => <option key={runtime} value={runtime}>{visible(agentLabel(runtime))}</option>)}</datalist>
        <span className="hint" id={`${id}-agent-hint`}>Enter a configured agent id. Loading choices checks it on the selected host.</span>
      </div>
      <label className="field context-path">Grant project path<input className="input" value={cwd} onChange={e => { resetContext(); setCwd(e.target.value) }} placeholder="Absolute path on this host" /></label>
    </div>
    <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={!online || !agent.trim() || !cwd.trim() || loading || hat.purging} onClick={() => void load()}>{loading ? 'Loading choices…' : 'Load grant choices'}</button>
    {[hosts.error, error].map((message, i) => message && <p role="alert" key={i}><Text>{message}</Text></p>)}
    {hosts.error && <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={hosts.reload}>Retry grant hosts</button>}
    {catalog && <>
      {!usable && <p role="alert">These choices are not current. Load grant choices again before adding values. Existing grants can still be removed.</p>}
      {!catalog.security.known && <p className="hint warn">This profile has no known safe baseline. Explicitly identify each policy setting and its allowed values. No safe settings are inferred.</p>}
      {fields.length === 0 && <p>No editable policy settings were reported.</p>}
      {fields.map(field => {
        const explicit = Object.hasOwn(runtimeGrants, field.key)
        const values = explicit ? runtimeGrants[field.key] : field.baseline
        const choices = policyChoices(field)
        return <fieldset className="grant-axis" key={field.key}><legend><Text>{field.option.name}</Text></legend>
          <p>Profile baseline: {field.baseline.length ? field.baseline.map(value => choices.find(c => c.value === value)?.label ?? String(value)).join(', ') : 'No allowed values reported'}</p>
          <label className="policy-control"><input type="checkbox" checked={explicit} disabled={!usable} onChange={e => setAllowed(replaceGrant(allowed, agent, field.key, e.target.checked ? [...field.baseline] : null))} />{catalog.security.known ? 'Override' : 'Use as policy setting'} <Text>{field.option.name}</Text></label>
          <div className="policy-values">{choices.map(choice => <label key={`${typeof choice.value}:${choice.value}`} className="policy-control"><input type="checkbox" checked={values.includes(choice.value)} disabled={!usable || !explicit} onChange={e => setAllowed(replaceGrant(allowed, agent, field.key, e.target.checked ? [...values, choice.value] : values.filter(value => value !== choice.value)))} /><Text>{choice.label}</Text></label>)}</div>
          {explicit && values.some(value => !choices.some(c => c.value === value)) && <p className="hint warn">Some saved values are unavailable in this context. They stay granted until you remove them below.</p>}
        </fieldset>
      })}
    </>}
    <div className="grant-review"><h4>All explicit grants for <Text>{hat.name}</Text></h4><GrantSummary allowed={allowed} catalog={catalog} />
      {Object.entries(allowed).flatMap(([runtime, axes]) => Object.keys(axes).map(axis => <button className="btn btn-ghost" key={`${runtime}:${axis}`} disabled={hat.purging} onClick={() => setAllowed(replaceGrant(allowed, runtime, axis, null))}>Remove override: <Text>{agentLabel(runtime)}</Text> / <Text>{grantField(catalog, runtime, axis)?.option.name ?? (axis === 'mode' ? 'Permissions' : axis)}</Text></button>))}
    </div>
    <p className="hint warn">Grants may let agents work with fewer permission prompts. Review every runtime and setting before saving. Launcher defaults do not grant delegation permissions.</p>
    <div className="card-actions"><button className="btn btn-primary" disabled={!changed || hat.purging} onClick={() => setReview(true)}>Review execution grants</button><button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={!changed} onClick={() => setAllowed(saved.allowed)}>Discard grant edits</button></div>
    {review && <ConfirmDialog title="Save execution grants?" confirm="Save execution grants" returnFocus={() => document.getElementById('delegation-heading')} onClose={() => setReview(false)} action={async () => onSaved(await setExecutionPolicy(client, hat.id, { allowed }))}>
      <p>Replace the complete execution grants for <Text>{hat.name}</Text>. These settings can reduce permission prompts. Other runtimes and settings are included below.</p>
      <h3>Saved grants</h3><GrantSummary allowed={saved.allowed} catalog={catalog} /><h3>Proposed grants</h3><GrantSummary allowed={allowed} catalog={catalog} />
    </ConfirmDialog>}
  </div>
}
function grantField(catalog: HostCatalog | null, agent: string, axis: string) {
  return catalog?.agent === agent ? policyFields(catalog).find(field => field.key === axis) : undefined
}
function GrantSummary({ allowed, catalog }: { allowed: ExecutionPolicy['allowed']; catalog: HostCatalog | null }) {
  return Object.keys(allowed).length === 0 ? <p>No explicit overrides. Known profiles use their baseline.</p> : <ul className="purge-list">{Object.entries(allowed).flatMap(([agent, axes]) => Object.entries(axes).map(([axis, values]) => {
    const field = grantField(catalog, agent, axis)
    const label = (value: (typeof values)[number]) => field ? policyChoices(field).find(choice => choice.value === value)?.label ?? `${String(value)} (not offered here)` : typeof value === 'boolean' ? value ? 'On' : 'Off' : value
    return <li key={`${agent}:${axis}`}><Text>{agentLabel(agent)}</Text> · <Text>{field?.option.name ?? (axis === 'mode' ? 'Permissions' : axis)}</Text>: <Text>{values.length ? values.map(label).join(', ') : 'No values allowed'}</Text></li>
  }))}</ul>
}
````
