Write `web/src/components/NotificationSettings.tsx`:

````tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { policies as listPolicies, pushDevices, removePush, savePolicy, vapid } from '../api/device'
import { hats as listHats } from '../api/manage'
import { ApiFailure, messageOf } from '../api/errors'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import { useResource } from '../hooks/useResource'
import type { PushPolicyItem, PushSubscriptionItem } from '../generated/protocol'
import { Text } from '../lib/text'
import { BrowserPush, classifyPush, type PushState } from '../pwa/device'
import { pushJournal, type PushRecord } from '../pwa/journal'
import { registerWorker, usePwa } from '../pwa/lifecycle'
import { vapidBytes } from '../pwa/subscription'
import '../manage.css'

const COPY: Record<PushState, string> = {
  unsupported: 'This browser does not support notifications. Install the app where supported.',
  insecure: 'Notifications require a secure HTTPS connection.',
  blocked: 'Notifications are blocked by browser or OS settings. Change those settings to enable them.',
  off: 'Notifications are off on this browser.',
  unconfirmed: 'Browser registration is unconfirmed. Enable to register or repair it.',
  reenable: 'Enable again: the device was removed, its subscription changed, or collector keys changed.',
  signed_out: 'This push registration belongs to an expired login. Explicitly enable to bind it to this login.',
  enabled: 'Notifications are enabled on this browser.',
}
const supported = () => 'serviceWorker' in navigator && !!navigator.locks && typeof PushManager !== 'undefined' && typeof Notification !== 'undefined'
const hintDismissed = () => { try { return !!sessionStorage.getItem('hennery.install-hint-dismissed') } catch { return false } }

export default function NotificationSettings({ push, sessions }: { push: boolean; sessions: boolean }) {
  const client = useClient(), pwa = usePwa()
  const [devices, setDevices] = useState<PushSubscriptionItem[]>([])
  const [key, setKey] = useState('')
  const privacy = useResource(async () => { if (!sessions) return { hats: [], policies: [] }; const [hats, policies] = await Promise.all([listHats(client), listPolicies(client)]); return { hats, policies } }, [client, sessions])
  const [inspection, setInspection] = useState<{ subscription: PushSubscription | null; record: PushRecord; matches: boolean } | null>(null)
  const [checked, setChecked] = useState<number | null>(null), [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null), [busy, setBusy] = useState(false), [prepared, setPrepared] = useState(false)
  const [label, setLabel] = useState(''), [hint, setHint] = useState(!hintDismissed())
  const device = useMemo(() => pwa.registration && key ? new BrowserPush(client, pwa.registration, pushJournal(), key) : null, [client, pwa.registration, key])

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      if (push) {
        const observed = pwa.registration && supported() ? await pushJournal().read() : null
        const [rows, loadedKey] = await Promise.all([pushDevices(client), vapid(client)])
        vapidBytes(loadedKey.public_key)
        if (pwa.registration && supported()) {
          const inspected = await new BrowserPush(client, pwa.registration, pushJournal(), loadedKey.public_key).reconcile(rows, fetch, observed?.generation)
          if (inspected.stale) throw new Error('Another window changed notification settings; refresh to check them.')
          setInspection(inspected)
        }
        setDevices(rows); setKey(loadedKey.public_key)
      }
      setChecked(Date.now()); setError(null)
    } catch (err) { setError('Unable to check device settings: ' + messageOf(err)) }
  }, [client, push, sessions, pwa.registration])
  useEffect(() => { void refresh() }, [refresh])

  const run = async (action: () => Promise<void>) => {
    setBusy(true); setError(null); setNotice(null)
    try { await action() } catch (err) { setError(messageOf(err)) } finally { setBusy(false) }
  }
  const state = classifyPush({ supported: supported(), secure: globalThis.isSecureContext === true,
    permission: typeof Notification === 'undefined' ? 'default' : Notification.permission,
    subscribed: !!inspection?.subscription, matches: inspection?.matches ?? true,
    record: inspection?.record ?? { generation: 0, enabled: false }, row: devices.find((r) => r.id === inspection?.record.id) })
  const canEnable = !['unsupported', 'insecure', 'blocked'].includes(state) && !!device
  const iosTab = /iPad|iPhone|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgent) && !window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches && !(navigator as Navigator & { standalone?: boolean }).standalone
  const expires = inspection?.subscription?.expirationTime
  const renewable = state === 'enabled' && expires != null && expires <= Date.now() + 86400000

  return <>
    {error && <p role="alert" className="form-error"><Text>{error}</Text>{checked && <> Last checked {new Date(checked).toLocaleString()}.</>}</p>}
    {notice && <p role="status">{notice}</p>}
    <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(refresh)}>Refresh settings</button>
    {push && <section className="card" aria-labelledby="browser-push-heading">
      <h2 id="browser-push-heading">Notifications on this browser</h2>
      {hint && <div><p>Install hennery for convenient access. Enable notifications only when you want them.</p>
        <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => { try { sessionStorage.setItem('hennery.install-hint-dismissed', '1') } catch { /* Nonessential preference. */ } setHint(false) }}>Dismiss install hint</button></div>}
      {iosTab && <p>Use your browser’s Share or menu control, Add to Home Screen, then launch that app and sign in there if needed before enabling notifications.</p>}
      <p role="status">{inspection === null && !['unsupported', 'insecure', 'blocked'].includes(state) ? 'Notification registration has not been verified.' : COPY[state]}</p>
      {!pwa.registration && !pwa.error && supported() && globalThis.isSecureContext === true && <p role="status">Preparing the offline shell…</p>}
      {pwa.error && <p role="alert">This browser could not prepare offline access. <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => void registerWorker()}>Retry worker setup</button></p>}
      <label className="field">Device label (optional)<input className="input" value={label} maxLength={64} onChange={(e) => setLabel(e.target.value)} /></label>
      <div className="card-actions">
        {canEnable && (prepared ? <button className="btn btn-primary" disabled={busy} onClick={() => {
          // The permission API is invoked in this tap before ANY await.
          const permission = Notification.permission === 'granted' ? Promise.resolve('granted') : Notification.requestPermission()
          void run(async () => {
            if (await permission !== 'granted') { setPrepared(false); setNotice('Notifications were not enabled. Check browser or OS settings.'); return }
            await device!.enable(label.trim() || undefined); setPrepared(false); await refresh()
          })
        }}>Enable notifications</button> : <button className="btn btn-primary" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(async () => { await client.confirm(); setPrepared(true) })}>Prepare notifications</button>)}
        <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy || !device} onClick={() => void run(async () => {
          const result = await device!.disable(); setPrepared(false)
          setNotice(result.server && result.browser ? 'Notifications disabled.' : !result.server && !result.browser ? 'Collector removal and browser cleanup are unconfirmed. Retry Disable.' : !result.server ? 'Browser unsubscribed; collector removal is unconfirmed. Retry Disable.' : 'Collector registration removed; browser cleanup is unconfirmed. Retry Disable.')
          await refresh()
        })}>Disable notifications</button>
        {renewable && <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(async () => { await device!.renew(devices); await refresh() })}>Renew subscription</button>}
      </div>
      <p className="hint">The icon does not guarantee offline storage. An offline first visit needs a successful installation first. Receiving a push does not make the collector reachable.</p>
    </section>}
    {push && <section className="card"><h2>Push devices</h2><p className="hint">These are registrations, not unique physical devices. Removing one stops delivery; it does not sign that browser out.</p>
      <ul className="cards">{devices.map((row) => <li className="card" key={row.id}>
        <h3><Text>{row.device_label}</Text>{row.id === inspection?.record.id && ' (this registration)'}</h3>
        <dl className="facts"><dt>Endpoint host</dt><dd><Text>{row.endpoint_host}</Text></dd><dt>Created</dt><dd>{row.created_at}</dd>
          <dt>Last service acceptance</dt><dd>{row.last_success_at ?? 'None reported'}</dd><dt>Delivery error</dt><dd><Text>{row.last_error ?? 'None reported'}</Text></dd></dl>
        {row.signed_out && <p>Signed out or expired; this registration can still receive push.</p>}
        <p className="hint">Service acceptance does not prove the OS displayed or you read a notification.</p>
        <button className="btn btn-ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(async () => {
          if (row.id === inspection?.record.id && device) {
            const result = await device.disable()
            if (!result.server || !result.browser) setNotice('Removal or browser cleanup is unconfirmed. Retry Disable.')
          } else await removePush(client, { id: row.id })
          await refresh()
        })}>Remove <Text>{row.device_label}</Text></button>
      </li>)}</ul>
    </section>}
    {sessions && <section className="card" aria-labelledby="privacy-heading"><h2 id="privacy-heading">Notification privacy by hat</h2><p>These settings apply to every push device for this hat. Question details are agent text and may contain secrets. A generic title with details still reveals that text.</p>
      {privacy.error && <p role="alert">Unable to load notification privacy. <Text>{privacy.error}</Text> <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={privacy.reload}>Retry privacy</button></p>}
      {!privacy.data && !privacy.error && <p role="status">Loading notification privacy…</p>}
      {(privacy.data?.policies ?? []).map(policy => <Policy key={policy.hat_id} policy={policy} name={privacy.data?.hats.find(h => h.id === policy.hat_id)?.name ?? 'Unavailable hat'} onRemove={privacy.reload} />)}
    </section>}
  </>

}

function Policy({ policy, name, onRemove }: { policy: PushPolicyItem; name: string; onRemove: () => void }) {
  const client = useClient(), [value, setValue] = useState(policy), [busy, setBusy] = useState(false), [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  return <fieldset className="card" disabled={busy}><legend><Text>{name}</Text></legend>
    {(['muted', 'generic_title', 'details'] as const).map((key, i) => <label className="policy-control" key={key}>
      <input type="checkbox" checked={value[key]} onChange={(e) => setValue({ ...value, [key]: e.target.checked })} />{['Mute', 'Use a generic title', 'Include question details'][i]}
    </label>)}
    <p>Preview: {value.muted ? 'No notification' : value.generic_title ? 'hennery needs your answer' : 'Example project needs your answer'}{!value.muted && value.details && ' — Example question text (may contain secrets)'}</p>
    {error && <p role="alert"><Text>{error}</Text></p>}
    <button className="btn btn-ghost" onClick={() => {
      setBusy(true); setError(null)
      const { muted, details, generic_title } = value
      void savePolicy(client, policy.hat_id, { muted, details, generic_title }).then(setValue, (err: unknown) => {
        if (err instanceof ApiFailure && err.status === 404) onRemove()
        else setError(messageOf(err))
      }).finally(() => setBusy(false))
    }}>Save policy</button>
  </fieldset>
}
````

Write `web/src/components/Pairing.tsx`:

````tsx
// "Add host" (frontend spec §8, kernel spec §4.1): a one-time pairing code,
// minted behind a step-up, and the exact command that pairs a machine with
// it, with a countdown to its expiry.
//
// The code is shown once. It lives in the Hosts screen's state and this
// panel only: never in the address bar, the title, storage or the console.
// It is dropped from the page when it expires, when a new host pairs, and
// when the panel closes or unmounts. Expired or paired, the code is spent:
// the panel tells Hosts, which drops it and offers "Add host" again while
// the panel still says what happened.
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { hosts as listHosts } from '../api/manage'
import { useClient } from '../app-client'
import type { HostItem } from '../generated/protocol'
import { Text } from '../lib/text'

/** A code is dead 600 s after minting (kernel spec §4.1): the countdown
 *  never shows more, whatever the clocks say. */
export const CODE_LIFETIME_S = 600
/** How often the host list is read while a code waits for its host. */
export const PAIRED_POLL_MS = 3000

/** What "Add host" got: the minted code, where hosts reach the collector,
 *  and the hosts paired before it, read just before the mint. */
export interface Minted {
  code: string
  publicUrl: string
  known: string[]
}

type Stage = { kind: 'live' } | { kind: 'expired' } | { kind: 'paired'; host: HostItem }

interface Props {
  /** `null` once the code is spent. */
  minted: Minted | null
  onPaired: () => void
  /** The code expired or a host paired with it: it is of no use now. */
  onSpent: () => void
  onClose: () => void
}

export default function Pairing({ minted, onPaired, onSpent, onClose }: Props) {
  const client = useClient()
  const [stage, setStage] = useState<Stage>({ kind: 'live' })
  // Counted from the answer's arrival, on the monotonic clock: the server's
  // `expires_at` is minted on its clock, and this browser's may be off by
  // more than the lifetime. The code dies within the network's delay of
  // this.
  const [deadline] = useState(() => performance.now() + CODE_LIFETIME_S * 1000)
  const [left, setLeft] = useState(CODE_LIFETIME_S)
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const before = useRef(new Set(minted?.known))
  // The parent's callbacks, current at each tick, without restarting the
  // poll whenever the parent renders.
  const told = useRef({ onPaired, onSpent })
  told.current = { onPaired, onSpent }

  // The countdown, and the code's end at zero.
  const counting = stage.kind === 'live'
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!counting) return
    const tick = () => {
      const s = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((deadline - performance.now()) / 1000))
      setLeft(s)
      if (s === 0) {
        setStage({ kind: 'expired' })
        told.current.onSpent()
      }
    }
    tick()
    const timer = setInterval(tick, 1000)
    return () => clearInterval(timer)
  }, [counting, deadline])

  // A host that pairs while the code is live ends it here: the code is
  // spent. A tick while a read is still out is skipped, so reads never
  // pile up on a slow link.
  const waiting = stage.kind === 'live'
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!waiting) return
    let live = true
    let reading = false
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      if (reading) return
      reading = true
      listHosts(client).then(
        (list) => {
          reading = false
          const fresh = list.find((h) => !before.current.has(h.host_id))
          if (live && fresh) {
            setStage({ kind: 'paired', host: fresh })
            told.current.onSpent()
            told.current.onPaired()
          }
        },
        () => {
          // A failed read is tried again at the next tick.
          reading = false
        },
      )
    }, PAIRED_POLL_MS)
    return () => {
      live = false
      clearInterval(timer)
    }
  }, [client, waiting])

  return (
    <section className="card pairing" aria-labelledby="pairing-title">
      <h2 id="pairing-title" className="card-title">
        Add a host
      </h2>
      {stage.kind === 'live' && minted && (
        <div className="pairing-live">
          <div className="pairing-summary">
            <h3>Pairing code</h3>
            <p className="pairing-code"><code>{minted.code}</code></p>
            <p>Valid for <span role="timer" aria-live="off">{formatLeft(left)}</span>, and once only.</p>
            <p role="status" className="hint">Waiting for the host… Closing this hides the code; it stays valid until it expires.</p>
          </div>
          <div className="pairing-instructions">
          <ol className="pair-steps"><li>Open a terminal on the new machine with hennery installed.</li><li>Run the command below, then start the host service.</li></ol>
          <pre className="command" aria-label="Pairing command">
            <code>{`hennery host join ${minted.publicUrl} ${minted.code}`}</code>
          </pre>
          <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onClick={() => {
            if (!navigator.clipboard?.writeText) { setCopied('Copy unavailable. Select the command above to copy it.'); return }
            void navigator.clipboard.writeText(`hennery host join ${minted.publicUrl} ${minted.code}`).then(() => setCopied('Command copied.'), () => setCopied('Copy unavailable. Select the command above to copy it.'))
          }}>Copy command</button>
          {copied && <p role="status">{copied}</p>}
          <p className="hint">
            Leave the code out and <code>host join</code> asks for it instead, which keeps it out of your shell
            history.
          </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {stage.kind === 'expired' && <p role="status">The code has expired. Add a host again for a new one.</p>}
      {stage.kind === 'paired' && (
        <p role="status">
          Paired: <Text>{stage.host.name}</Text>
        </p>
      )}
      <div className="card-actions">
        <button type="button" className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onClick={onClose}>
          Close
        </button>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

/** `m:ss`. */
export function formatLeft(seconds: number): string {
  const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(seconds))
  return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`
}
````

Write `web/src/lib/delegationPolicy.test.ts`:

````typescript
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import type { HostCatalog } from '../generated/protocol'
import { policyChoices, policyFields, replaceGrant } from './delegationPolicy'
const base: HostCatalog = { agent: 'custom', adapter_revision: '1', freshness: 'fresh', defaults: {}, security: { known: true, axes: { mode: ['safe'], sandbox: [false] } }, config_options: [
  { id: 'permission', category: 'mode', name: 'Permissions', type: 'select', currentValue: 'safe', options: [{ value: 'safe', name: 'Ask first' }] },
  { id: 'sandbox', type: 'boolean', currentValue: false },
  { id: 'effort', type: 'boolean', currentValue: false },
  { id: 'model', category: 'model', type: 'select', currentValue: 'one', options: [{ value: 'one', name: 'One' }] },
] }
describe('execution policy catalog mapping', () => {
  it('uses trusted classification and maps mode category to wire identity, never a display label', () => {
    expect(policyFields(base).map(f => f.key)).toEqual(['mode', 'sandbox'])
    expect(policyChoices(policyFields(base)[0])).toEqual([{ value: 'safe', label: 'Ask first' }])
    expect(policyChoices(policyFields(base)[1])).toEqual([{ value: false, label: 'Off' }, { value: true, label: 'On' }])
  })
  it('unknown profiles offer explicit policy identification with empty baseline and no model grant', () => {
    expect(policyFields({ ...base, security: { known: false, axes: {} } }).map(f => [f.key, f.baseline])).toEqual([['mode', []], ['effort', []], ['sandbox', []]])
  })
  it('never infers baseline values for an unknown profile even when axes are reported', () => {
    expect(policyFields({ ...base, security: { known: false, axes: base.security.axes } }).every(field => field.baseline.length === 0)).toBe(true)
  })
  it('preserves unrelated grants, explicit false and empty denial; removal restores inheritance', () => {
    const allowed = { other: { sandbox: [false], mode: ['safe'] } }
    const updated = replaceGrant(allowed, 'agent', 'mode', [])
    expect(updated).toEqual({ ...allowed, agent: { mode: [] } })
    expect(replaceGrant(updated, 'agent', 'mode', null)).toEqual(allowed)
    expect(allowed).toEqual({ other: { sandbox: [false], mode: ['safe'] } })
    expect(replaceGrant(allowed, 'other', 'mode', null)).toEqual({ other: { sandbox: [false] } })
  })
  it('treats opaque object-property ids as data without prototype mutation', () => {
    const updated = replaceGrant({}, '__proto__', 'constructor', [false])
    expect(Object.keys(updated)).toEqual(['__proto__'])
    expect(Object.hasOwn(updated.__proto__, 'constructor')).toBe(true)
    expect(Object.getPrototypeOf(updated)).toBe(Object.prototype)
    expect(replaceGrant(updated, '__proto__', 'constructor', null)).toEqual({})
  })
})
````

Write `web/src/lib/delegationPolicy.ts`:

````typescript
import type { ConfigValue, ExecutionPolicy, HostCatalog } from '../generated/protocol'
import { configOptions, type ConfigOption } from './catalog'

export interface PolicyField { key: string; option: ConfigOption; baseline: ConfigValue[] }
/** Use the same category/legacy-id ordering as the shared catalog parser. */
export function policyFields(catalog: HostCatalog): PolicyField[] {
  const options = configOptions({ session_id: '', config_options: catalog.config_options, commands: [] })
  const mode = options.find(o => o.category === 'mode') ?? options.find(o => o.id === 'mode' && (!o.category || o.category.startsWith('_')))
  const model = options.find(o => o.category === 'model') ?? options.find(o => o.id === 'model' && (!o.category || o.category.startsWith('_')))
  return options.filter(o => o !== model).flatMap(option => {
    const key = option === mode ? 'mode' : option.id
    if (catalog.security.known && !Object.hasOwn(catalog.security.axes, key)) return []
    return [{ key, option, baseline: catalog.security.known ? catalog.security.axes[key] ?? [] : [] }]
  })
}
export const policyChoices = (field: PolicyField): { value: ConfigValue; label: string }[] => field.option.kind === 'boolean'
  ? [{ value: false, label: 'Off' }, { value: true, label: 'On' }]
  : field.option.choices.map(choice => ({ value: choice.value, label: choice.name }))

/** A deliberate per-axis edit preserves every unrelated runtime and setting. */
export function replaceGrant(allowed: ExecutionPolicy['allowed'], agent: string, key: string, values: ConfigValue[] | null): ExecutionPolicy['allowed'] {
  const next = structuredClone(allowed)
  if (values === null) {
    if (Object.hasOwn(next, agent)) {
      delete next[agent][key]
      if (Object.keys(next[agent]).length === 0) delete next[agent]
    }
  } else {
    // Object.fromEntries also handles opaque runtime/axis ids such as __proto__.
    const axes = Object.hasOwn(next, agent) ? next[agent] : {}
    return Object.fromEntries([...Object.entries(next).filter(([id]) => id !== agent), [agent, Object.fromEntries([...Object.entries(axes).filter(([id]) => id !== key), [key, values]])]])
  }
  return next
}
````

/* The management screens (plan 4d): Hosts, Hats and Settings. Their own
   file, so the shell's index.css stays the shell's. Mobile-first: one
   column, every target at least 44 px under 768 px. */

.manage { width:100%; max-width:860px; margin:0 auto; padding:24px 20px calc(32px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom)); display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:16px; }
.manage-head { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:12px; }
.manage-head h1 { font-family:var(--font-display); font-weight:500; font-size:26px; letter-spacing:-.01em; margin:0; flex:1; }
.cards { list-style:none; margin:0; padding:0; display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:12px; }
.card { background:var(--surface); border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:var(--r-md); box-shadow:var(--sh-sm); padding:16px 18px; min-width:0; }
.card-head { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px; flex-wrap:wrap; margin-bottom:10px; }
.card-title { font-family:var(--font-display); font-weight:700; font-size:16px; margin:0; min-width:0; overflow-wrap:anywhere; flex:1; }
.card-actions { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; flex-wrap:wrap; margin-top:12px; }
.card-actions .spacer { flex:1; }
.card p { margin:0 0 10px; overflow-wrap:anywhere; }
.notice { border-color:var(--accent); }

.facts { display:grid; grid-template-columns:max-content minmax(0,1fr); gap:4px 14px; margin:0; font-size:14px; }
.facts dt { color:var(--fg-muted); }
.facts dd { margin:0; min-width:0; overflow-wrap:anywhere; }

.state, .tag { font-size:11px; font-weight:700; letter-spacing:.06em; text-transform:uppercase; border-radius:var(--r-full); padding:3px 9px; white-space:nowrap; }
.state-online { background:var(--st-run-bg); color:var(--st-run-fg); }
.state-offline { background:var(--surface-3); color:var(--fg-muted); }
.state-revoked { background:var(--st-fail-bg); color:var(--st-attn); }
.host-revoked .card-title { color:var(--fg-muted); }
.tag { background:var(--accent-wash); color:var(--accent); }
.tag-warn { background:var(--st-warn-bg); color:var(--st-warn-fg); }

.swatch { width:18px; height:18px; border-radius:6px; flex:none; background:var(--fg-quiet); border:1px solid var(--border-strong); }
.colour-input { width:52px; height:40px; padding:2px; border:1.5px solid var(--border-strong); border-radius:var(--r-sm); background:var(--surface-2); }

.inline-form { display:flex; align-items:flex-end; gap:10px; flex-wrap:wrap; margin-top:8px; }
.inline-form .field { margin:0; flex:1; min-width:180px; }
.select-field { display:inline-flex; flex-direction:column; gap:4px; }
.select-field .field-label { margin:0; font-size:12px; }
.select-field select { min-height:36px; border:1.5px solid var(--border-strong); border-radius:var(--r-sm); background:var(--surface-2); color:var(--fg-1); padding:6px 8px; font:inherit; max-width:100%; }

.command { background:var(--hn-term-bg); color:var(--hn-term-fg); border-radius:var(--r-sm); padding:12px 14px; margin:0 0 10px; overflow-x:auto; font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:13px; }
.pairing-code code { font-family:var(--font-mono); font-weight:700; }
.hint { color:var(--fg-muted); font-size:13px; }
.empty { color:var(--fg-muted); }
.pwa-banner { margin:0; padding:10px 16px; background:var(--surface-2); color:var(--fg-1); border-bottom:1px solid var(--border); }
.policy-control { display:flex; gap:10px; align-items:center; min-height:44px; }
.device-settings .btn { min-height:44px; }
.device-settings input.input { width:100%; min-height:44px; }
.sign-out-error { margin-top:8px; font-size:13px; }

.rules { margin-top:12px; }
.rule-list { list-style:none; margin:0; padding:0; display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:10px; }
.rule { display:flex; align-items:flex-end; gap:10px; flex-wrap:wrap; }

@media (max-width:767px) {

/* MCP (plan 4e): the connection form, the token and the mounts. */
.connection .card-actions .btn[aria-disabled="true"] { opacity:.45; box-shadow:none; transform:none; cursor:not-allowed; }
.connection-form { display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:10px; margin-top:8px; }
.connection-form .field, .connection-form .hint { margin:0; }
.field-row { display:flex; gap:10px; flex-wrap:wrap; }

.check { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; min-height:36px; cursor:pointer; }
.check input { width:18px; height:18px; margin:0; flex:none; }
.hint.warn { color:var(--st-warn-fg); }

.mounts legend { font-weight:700; font-size:13px; padding:0; margin-bottom:6px; }
.mcp-ok { background:var(--st-run-bg); color:var(--st-run-fg); }
